### [1] [Shaping black hole resonances II: Spectroscopic landscapes and interference in black hole ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13075)
*Alejandro Svyatkovskyy Kholyavka,Sayak Datta,Jose Antonio León Vega,Xisco Jiménez Forteza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解析地给出了高斯型振荡扰动激发Schwarzschild黑洞准正规模（QNM）激发系数$C_n$随脉冲宽度$\sigma$与载频$\nu$的变化规律，指出QNM激发不能仅靠频率匹配理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为高效激发QNM需载频与模式频率匹配，但缺乏对任意脉宽与载频下激发规律的解析刻画，需厘清扰动频谱结构与QNM复频率的耦合机制。

Method: 解析地将激发系数分解为本征激发因子与源依赖的重叠积分，重叠积分在复QNM频率处取值为正负频支的相干叠加，其幅度严格分解为高斯谱包络与干涉因子；并与时域数值模拟对比验证。

Result: 仅窄带源在$\nu=\omega_n^{\rm Re}$处激发峰值；宽带脉冲最大激发出现在$\nu=0$。干涉仅在$\nu\neq0$时调制幅度，调制显著度由$\omega_n^{\rm Re}/\omega_n^{\rm Im}$控制。相位分为模相关项、宽度项与$\nu$依赖项。数值验证：$|C_0|$中位相对误差约4%；$|C_1|$在$\sigma\leq5$时约16%，全参数空间约36%；相位中位偏差$n=0$约0.14 rad，$n=1$、$\sigma\leq5$约0.34 rad。

Conclusion: QNM激发由源谱与复频率重叠的相干干涉决定，而非单纯频率匹配；该解析框架可为更真实的天体物理场景提供定性洞见。

Abstract: We map analytically the excitation of Schwarzschild quasinormal modes (QNMs) $C_n$ by oscillatory Gaussian perturbations across the pulse width $σ$ and carrier frequency $ν$. The excitation coefficient of each mode is determined by the product of an intrinsic excitation factor and a source-dependent overlap evaluated at the complex QNM frequency. For oscillatory Gaussian perturbations, this overlap is the coherent sum of the positive- and negative-frequency branches of the source, with its amplitude factorizing exactly into a Gaussian spectral envelope and an interference factor. We observe that the excitation amplitude peaks at $ν=ω_n^{\rm Re}$ only for narrowband sources. For sufficiently broadband pulses, the maximum lies at $ν=0$, so efficient excitation does not necessarily require matching the carrier and the mode frequency. We observe that the interference between branches, modulates the amplitude only for nonzero $ν$, a feature generic to real oscillatory sources. The prominence of the interference-induced modulation is controlled by the ratio $ω_n^{\rm Re}/ω_n^{\rm Im}$. The phase separates into a mode-only, a width-only and a $ν$-dependent term, the last generated by the same interference, with $ν=0$ being its unique maximum at fixed width. The analytical predictions are validated against time-domain numerical simulations, showing a relative median error of $\sim 4\%$ for $|C_0|$. For $|C_1|$, the median error is $\sim 16\%$ when $σ\leq 5$, increasing to $\sim 36\%$ across the full parameter space. The median phase difference for $n=0$ is $\sim0.14 \,\rm rad $ and for $n=1$, $σ\leq5$ it is $0.34\,\rm rad$. Our results demonstrate that QNM excitation cannot be understood through frequency matching alone and may provide qualitative insights into more realistic astrophysical scenarios.

</details>


### [2] [Depletion of Dark Matter Spikes by Massive Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13170)
*Charlie Sharpe,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Ricarda S. Beckmann,Alexander Heger,Bence Kocsis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 大质量黑洞并合会显著破坏暗物质尖峰：即使质量比q=0.03的并合也能在100(5)史瓦西半径处降低密度约3(5)个数量级；结合Horizon-AGN模拟，最终把可探测暗物质尖峰的质量范围限制在10^6–5×10^7 M⊙（z≤3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质尖峰导致IMRI/EMRI引力波退相位，是间接探测暗物质的诱人通道，但该通道要求尖峰在宇宙学时间尺度上存活。已有工作多关注EMRI引起的耗竭，而宿主黑洞的并合历史对尖峰的影响尚不清楚，需要量化并合对尖峰的破坏程度并评估实际存活概率。

Method: 数值模拟大质量双黑洞旋近对中心约100史瓦西半径内暗物质尖峰的改造与破坏，考察不同质量比q、不同极角及主黑洞质量的影响；并结合Horizon-AGN宇宙学模拟数据，统计给定红移下发生不同质量比并合的大质量黑洞比例，同时分析吸积引起的绝热增长能否在两次并合之间使尖峰重新生长。

Result: 质量比q=0.03的单次并合即可在100(5)史瓦西半径处、相对双星轨道面各极角上使尖峰密度降低约3(5)个数量级，且与主黑洞质量无关；更大质量比破坏更强；q低至0.005的并合也降低中心密度2–3个数量级，使退相位难以探测。Horizon-AGN数据显示并合随黑洞质量增加而更普遍：q>0.03的并合在z=0时分别发生于50%的3×10^7 M⊙和95%的10^8 M⊙黑洞，在z=3时对应质量降至2×10^7 M⊙和5×10^7 M⊙；吸积诱导的绝热增长不足以在并合间实现可观的尖峰再生长。

Conclusion: 并合历史是暗物质尖峰存活的关键限制因素，与以往EMRI耗竭研究结合，可将可承载可探测暗物质尖峰的大质量黑洞质量范围约束为10^6–5×10^7 M⊙（z≤3）。

Abstract: Dark matter over-densities around massive black holes, known as dark matter spikes, can dephase gravitational waves emitted by intermediate- and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs/EMRIs), offering a promising way to indirectly detect dark matter. This detection channel, however, requires the spike to survive over cosmological time-scales. We investigate the impact that a host black hole's merger history has on its dark-matter spike. We focus on how the inspiral of a massive-black-hole (MBH) binary modifies and disrupts these spikes within the central $\sim 100$ Schwarzschild radii of the black hole. We find that a single merger with mass ratio $q = 0.03$ is capable of reducing a spike's density by around 3 (5) orders of magnitude at 100 (5) Schwarzschild radii across all polar angles relative to the binary's plane, independent of the primary MBH's mass, with larger mass ratios inducing even stronger depletion. In fact, a single past merger with mass ratio as small as $q = 0.005$ reduces central densities by 2--3 orders of magnitude, making gravitational-wave dephasing difficult to detect. We relate these results to the MBH merger history by analysing cosmological simulation data from Horizon-AGN. We find that mergers become increasingly common as the MBH mass increases, with mergers of mass ratio q > 0.03 having occurred in 50% of MBHs of mass $3\times10^7M_\odot$ and 95% of mass $10^8M_\odot$ at z=0. These fractions are reached at even lighter masses ($2\times10^7M_\odot$ and $5\times10^7M_\odot$, respectively) at z=3. We find that accretion-induced adiabatic MBH growth is unable to cause appreciable spike re-growth between such mergers in {\tt Horizon-AGN}. Thus, in combination with previous studies of EMRI-induced depletion, this work constrains the mass range over which massive black holes can host detectable DM spikes to $10^6M_\odot$--$5 \times 10^7M_\odot$ for $z \leq 3$.

</details>


### [3] [Eccentricity greatly enhances the detectability of line-of-sight acceleration in gravitational wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13197)
*János Takátsy,Anna Puecher,Gonzalo Morras,Lorenz Zwick*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 偏心率显著增强引力波信号对视线加速度（LSA）等环境效应的敏感性，关键在于从首个可探测的偏心谐波进入探测带时（而非惯用的 n=2 谐波）开始分析信号，从而探测更长的早期旋进段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学形成的致密双星具有偏心率尾巴并可能受环境扰动。作者希望利用偏心率这一特征提升对视线加速度（LSA）这类环境效应的探测能力，突破传统以 n=2 谐波入带时刻为起点的分析限制。

Method: 以 Fisher 矩阵预言为初步工具，进一步用最先进的偏心波形模型做全贝叶斯参数估计，验证增强效应是否在完整推断中保留；核心做法是将信号分析起点设为第一个可探测偏心谐波进入探测带的时刻。

Result: 增强效应在 Fisher 分析中可复现，并在全贝叶斯参数估计中持续存在。对信噪比充足的信号，e20 从 0 增至约 0.1 时 LSA 约束可提升一个数量级以上；即使低信噪比信号也预期有约 5 倍放大。

Conclusion: 充分利用该效应需要持续改进高效偏心波形模型与似然评估，以高效分析持续数千秒、含大量谐波的信号。

Abstract: Compact object binaries formed dynamically are characterized by a tail of eccentric sources and by the potential presence of environmental perturbations. Here we show that the presence of eccentricity in a gravitational-wave (GW) signal strongly enhances the sensitivity to environmental effects, focusing in particular on line-of-sight acceleration (LSA). We demonstrate that this enhancement is recovered in Fisher-analysis forecasts and, crucially, persists in full Bayesian parameter estimation using state-of-the-art eccentric waveform models. The key insight is that the signal should be analyzed from the moment the first detectable eccentric harmonic enters the detector band, rather than from the customary entry of the $n=2$ harmonic: this allows to probe the early inspiral and thus a longer signal, over which the GW dephasing introduced by LSA becomes substantially larger. We show that with this approach constraints on LSA can increase by more than an order of magnitude between eccentricities at 20~Hz $e_{20}=0$ and $e_{20}\sim0.1$, for signals with sufficient signal-to-noise-ratio (SNR), while we predict a factor $5$ amplification even for low-SNR ones. Fully exploiting this effect necessitates the continued improvement of efficient eccentric waveform models and likelihood evaluations, enabling the efficient analysis of signals lasting thousands of seconds and containing a large number of harmonics.

</details>

*Valentin O. Deliyski,Tsanimir Angelov,Galin N. Gyulchev,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了带同步标量毛的自洽Kerr黑洞周围几何薄、光学薄吸积盘间接像的偏振强度特性；发现强标量化模型中高阶（n≥2）中心环状像不被指数级压低，其偏振强度相对直接像显著，故观测特征不能仅由n=1像主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对标量毛黑洞阴影/像的讨论多集中于直接像与n=1像，且通常认为高阶像因指数级去放大而可忽略；作者希望检验在完全自洽的标量化Kerr时空中，大质量自引力标量场改变引力透镜性质后，高阶间接像的偏振贡献是否仍可忽略。

Method: 对完全自洽的带同步标量毛Kerr黑洞模型，取几何薄且光学薄的吸积盘，计算其间接像的强度与偏振分布；选取一个具代表性的高标量荷模型，在低观测倾角下考察高阶（n≥2）中心环状像的形态、去放大程度及相对直接像的偏振强度，并分析其对盘径向发射率剖面与倾角的依赖。

Result: 在代表性高标量荷模型、低倾角条件下出现高阶中心环状像；这些像未被指数级去放大，其偏振强度相对直接像显著。结论对盘的径向发射率剖面和观测倾角敏感。

Conclusion: 强标量化Kerr黑洞的观测特征可能并非仅由n=1像主导，n≥2像也可能对观测到的偏振辐射有显著贡献；因此高阶像在标量毛黑洞的偏振观测检验中不可忽视。

Abstract: We investigate the intensity polarization properties of the indirect images of a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk around fully self-consistent models of Kerr black holes with synchronized scalar hair. The presence of a massive, self-gravitating scalar field can dramatically alter the lensing properties of the spacetime, resulting in morphologically complex indirect images. We show that for a representative high scalar charge model, this results in the presence of high order central ring-like images at low observer inclinations. These images are not exponentially demagnified, and their polarized intensity, relative to the direct images, is significant. We therefore conclude that the observational signature of strongly scalarized Kerr black holes may not be dominated solely by the $n=1$ image, as images with $n\geq2$ may also contribute significantly to the observed polarized emission, depending on the radial emissivity profile of the disk and the observer inclination.

</details>


### [5] [Challenging the hoop conjecture with spinning pancakes and baguettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13266)
*Robin Croft,Gerardo García-Moreno,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过构造轴对称旋转尘埃初始数据族，检验了适用于旋转情形的“圈猜想”（hoop conjecture）表述，发现当准局域质量包含转动能时猜想以约14%以内的系数成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性定理只刻画稳态终态，奇点定理只保证测地线不完备，二者都未给出视界形成的一般判据；圈猜想将封闭质量与各方向上的物质分布致密度联系起来，可作为视界形成的判据，但其在旋转情形下的适用性尚需检验。

Method: 构造描述旋转尘埃的轴对称初始数据族，检验适配该情形的圈猜想表述；研究具有常值及微分重标度角速度剖面的高度长椭球与扁椭球构型，包括接近表观视界形成阈值的情形；比较包含转动能的准局域质量与表观视界的不可约质量两种质量定义，并区分无视界构型中物质面内所围能量与ADM质量。

Result: 对旋转构型，圈猜想成立，系数与非旋转情形一样在1的14%以内，前提是准局域质量包含转动能；若改用表观视界的不可约质量，偏差随自旋增大，与Kerr黑洞情形相同。对无视界构型，当质量取物质面所围能量时猜想成立；而使用ADM质量（额外包含物质外部的转动场能）则出现约25%的表观违反。

Conclusion: 圈猜想在旋转尘埃构型中成立，但其正确表述依赖于恰当的质量定义——须计入转动能且限于物质所围能量；文章还讨论了无表观视界的致密构型可能的演化、其与弱宇宙监督的联系及对无视界致密天体的启示。

Abstract: Understanding when gravitational collapse produces a black hole remains a central challenge in general relativity. Black-hole uniqueness results characterize the stationary endpoint under suitable assumptions, while singularity theorems establish geodesic incompleteness in the presence of trapped surfaces and appropriate energy and global conditions. Neither, however, provides a general criterion for horizon formation. The hoop conjecture offers such a criterion by relating the enclosed mass to the compactness of a matter distribution in every direction. We construct axisymmetric families of initial data describing rotating dust and test a formulation of the conjecture adapted to this setting. We explore highly prolate and oblate configurations with both constant and differential rescaled angular-velocity profiles, including configurations near the threshold for apparent-horizon formation. We find that the conjecture holds for rotating configurations, with a coefficient within 14% of unity as in the nonrotating case, provided the quasilocal mass includes the rotational energy. If the irreducible mass of the apparent horizon is used instead, the deviations grow with spin, as they do for Kerr black holes. For horizonless configurations, the conjecture likewise holds when the mass is restricted to the energy enclosed by the matter surface, whereas using the ADM mass, which also includes rotational field energy outside the matter, leads to apparent violations of up to $\approx$25%. We also discuss the possible evolution of compact configurations without apparent horizons, their relation to weak cosmic censorship, and their implications for horizonless compact objects.

</details>


### [6] [Viscosity heats, diffusion drains: Open-system thermodynamics in unimodular cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13269)
*Miguel Cruz,Samuel Lepe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在含体积粘性的广义相对论与含能量扩散的幺模引力中，物质部分均非绝热；当 Π↔Q̇/3H 时二者的平衡、熵与温度方程一致。是否"绝热"之争源于熵归属（仅物质 vs 总有效流体）。关键量是吸收扩散能量的扇区温度 T_D，共动熵产率为 Q̇(1/T_D−1/T_m)。有限 T_D 下扩散类似与外部热库的热交换，可使开放系统冷却，破坏粘性-扩散对应。宇宙学上 Π 由减速参数 q 固定、扩散由加加速度 j 固定，故 ΛCDM 是幺模宇宙学的绝热极限，却是粘性模型的耗散情形。含守恒静质量的气体扩散只移除热能、禁止 phantom 行为，并以有限热库约束宇宙学项增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清"幺模宇宙学是否绝热"的争论，并厘清体积粘性与能量扩散两种非绝热描述之间的对应关系及其适用范围（尤其是可观测性与稳定性方面）。

Method: 通过 Π↔Q̇/3H 建立两框架方程层面的映射；引入吸收能量扇区温度 T_D 计算共动熵产率 Q̇(1/T_D−1/T_m)，讨论 T_D→∞ 与 T_D→T_m 两个极限；以减速参数 q 约束 Π、以加加速度 j 约束扩散；用有效声速偏移 c_s²−ω 作为 |Π|/ρ 的扩散类比；并比较 Hayward–Kodama 视界温度下的广义第二定律。

Result: 有限 T_D 时扩散等价于与外库热交换，可冷却开放流体，从而在观测量与稳定性上打破粘性-扩散对应；ΛCDM 为幺模宇宙学的绝热极限，但作为粘性模型时仍具耗散性；扩散的稳定性由微扰条件决定而非加速度；静质量守恒的气体扩散仅移除热能、排除 phantom 行为，并以有限热库限制宇宙学项增长，产生对数气体型暗流体需 z≈12 的相对论性暗物质；幺模引力中广义第二定律可由局域第二定律推出，而体积粘性情形需另行施加。

Conclusion: 非绝热性应归于总有效流体而非仅物质；T_D 是区分粘性与扩散物理的核心参数，粘性-扩散对应仅在特定温度极限及特定观测量下成立。幺模引力的热力学一致性优于体积粘性模型。

Abstract: In GR with bulk viscosity and in unimodular gravity with energy diffusion, the matter sector is non-adiabatic, and their balance, entropy, and temperature equations match if $Π\leftrightarrow \dot{Q}/3H$. Disagreement over whether unimodular cosmology is adiabatic comes from assigning entropy to matter only or to the total effective fluid. The key is the temperature $T_D$ of the sector absorbing the diffused energy: comoving entropy production is $\dot{Q}(1/T_D-1/T_m)$, yielding earlier limits for $T_D \rightarrow \infty$ and $T_D \rightarrow T_m$. For finite $T_D$, diffusion acts like heat exchange with an external reservoir: unlike viscosity, which only heats a closed fluid, diffusion into a colder bath can cool an open one, breaking the viscosity-diffusion correspondence for observables and stability. Cosmographically, $Π$ is fixed by the deceleration $q$, while diffusion is fixed by the jerk $j$; thus $Λ$CDM is the adiabatic limit of unimodular cosmology but remains dissipative when modeled via viscosity. The diffusive analogue of $|Π|/ρ$ is the shift $c_s^2-ω$ in effective sound speed, set by perturbative stability rather than acceleration. If dark matter is a gas with conserved rest mass, consistent diffusion removes only thermal energy, forbids phantom behavior, and bounds the growth of the cosmological term by the finite thermal reservoir; producing a logotropic dark fluid with conserved particle number would require relativistic dark matter at $z\simeq12$. With the Hayward-Kodama apparent-horizon temperature, the generalized second law in unimodular gravity follows from the local one, whereas for bulk viscosity it must be imposed separately.

</details>


### [7] [The out-state of scalar-induced gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13462)
*Yizhou Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用经典LSZ型约化公式将标量诱导引力波（SIGW）中的自由辐射从不规范依赖的源响应中分离出来，并证明其振幅在任意外禀规范下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶张量扰动同时包含自由辐射与规范依赖的源响应，二者混在一起，导致"哪个量等于可观测的自由引力波"缺乏判据，尤其在不同规范（如宇宙学微扰论中的标准规范）下的结果是否一致需要严格证明。

This review presents a unified account of the Effective Metric Description (EMD), a model-independent framework in which deformations of static, spherically symmetric black hole spacetimes are parametrized by physical, coordinate-invariant quantities, such as the proper radial distance from the event horizon or the proper time of a free-falling observer. After reviewing the classical black hole solutions and the open issues that motivate going beyond GR, we develop the EMD as a series expansion near the horizon for a given physical quantity. We provide the general solution of the resulting self-consistency conditions compatible with the symmetries of the geometry, derive universal regularity conditions ensuring a finite Hawking temperature and curvature invariants, establish the equivalence of descriptions based on different physical quantities, and extend the framework to charged, extremal and lower-dimensional black holes. The framework is tested on a broad set of geometries used to model physics beyond GR, and compared with other model-independent parametrizations through explicit transformation maps. Abstract: Kinematic quantum geometry describes fast degrees of freedom (DOFs) that adiabatically follow states parametrized by both the coordinates and the velocities of slow DOFs. For any scheme of this class, the relaxation time is bounded below by the inverse fraction of pair configurations on which feedback acts; local feedback reaches $O(N_q)$ of $O(N_q^2)$ configurations, requiring a time at least linear in $N_q$. An exact second-moment closure determines the ensemble fluctuations: the measurement noise is conserving, while feedback generates removal shot noise and pair correlations. A photon counter with finite collection efficiency, gated time bins, and independent dark counts defines a common coarse-graining of the aligned trajectory laws. For fixed observation duration $\mathcal T$ and detector parameters, we prove that the observed relative entropy remains uniformly bounded as the bath temperature $T_b$ vanishes and is strictly positive at vacuum. The hidden contribution obeys $Σ_{\mathrm{hid}}(\mathcal T,T_b)=\mathcal T\dot Q_0/(k_BT_b)+O(1)$, where $\dot Q_0>0$ is the stationary vacuum heat current. Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) decoding has traditionally been organized around fixed decoding instances: a monolithic space-time detector error model (DEM), or a predetermined temporal window, is handed to a backend decoder after enough syndrome data has arrived. This paper presents FusionQ, a fusion decoding framework that changes this paradigm by deriving local decoding tasks from the observed syndrome and then fusing their results into a single global correction. Instead of forcing every shot through the same global or windowed problem, FusionQ builds open-boundary local DEMs around fired detector clusters and expands the corresponding footprints only when a local optimum uses virtual boundary errors. FusionQ is generic in the decoder backend: the scheduler requires only a local solution and a boundary-use flag, so exact solvers, matching-style decoders on graphlike DEMs, and heuristic decoders can share the same runtime. We prove a compositional optimality theorem: with an exact minimum-weight local backend and boundary-free publication, fusing the published local decoding results yields a global most-likely error (MLE) solution of the original DEM. Our evaluation shows that, with an exact backend, FusionQ matches the monolithic MLE result on every shot. Across decoder backends, FusionQ substantially reduces decoding latency by overlapping local decoding with hardware syndrome acquisition.

Abstract: Electrical solid-state qubits are generally initialized, manipulated and read out through diffusive conductors. It decays as the inverse square of the qubit separation and changes sign with the orientation of the pair, so that qubit pairs at equal separation may be correlated or anticorrelated, an unambiguous experimental signature. In two dimensions, the correlated noise a single qubit shares with the rest of the array, in the worst-case scenario, grows logarithmically with array size rather than converging. The sufficient condition for fault tolerance under correlated noise is therefore violated, so standard threshold theorems no longer guarantee protection against this channel for large qubit arrays. As this dephasing originates from the soft-mode content of the environment rather than on any specific gate detail, we expect correlated dephasing channels of this kind wherever solid-state qubits or sensors share a gapless conductive medium.

Abstract: Quantum processors now measure qubits in the middle of a circuit, and the stream of outcomes is the raw output of error correction, measurement-based state preparation and monitored dynamics. Abstract: An exceptional point of a memory-coefficient equation need not have the same signature in every quantum-state observable.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码能显著降低每个逻辑量子比特所需的数据比特数，但其校验涉及平面器件上的长程相互作用。如何在受硬件约束的平面/节点化硅器件上高效实现这些非局域校验，缺乏系统性的电路方案与布局层面评估，尤其不清楚"纠缠资源质量"与"传输方式"孰轻孰重。

Method: 1) 在硅节点网络上设计单态织物：在穿梭通道上产生自旋单态，在节点间分发，由局域操作消耗单态以测量非局域稳定子；2) 构造两种综合征提取电路——基于分布式GHZ态与基于ancilla隐形传态；3) 采用硬件约束的调度与电路级存储仿真进行评估；4) 与CAbLECAR（在各校验数据比特间搬运移动ancilla）对比，并在自定义节点格点上重排移动ancilla的走线；5) 考察比特翻转与退相位传输噪声、ancilla数据访问顺序固定、源预报（heralding）与相位纯化（purification）等变体。

Result: 在CAbLECAR原始布局上，当传输同时包含自旋翻转与退相位时，隐形传态可优于CAbLECAR。将移动ancilla置于节点格点上后，[[144,12,12]] Gross码的平均调度路径由350 μm缩短至153 μm，采样比特翻转区内的逻辑错误率最多降低50倍；在每个采样点上，直接传输均优于两种单态电路。在频繁传输比特翻转条件下，当双比特门与读出错误率降至约4–5×10⁻⁴（约为本文假设值的一半）以下时，GHZ电路反超直接传输。固定ancilla的数据访问顺序揭示出"更短路径"与"ancilla故障引发的关联错误"之间的权衡。源预报对两种单态电路均有改善；相位纯化仅在高于噪声相关交叉点时才有利于GHZ电路，且始终劣化隐形传态。

Conclusion: 物理布局与交互顺序对逻辑存储可靠性的影响可与综合征提取电路的选择相提并论；更"干净"的纠缠资源并不必然带来更可靠的逻辑存储。因此qLDPC码的硬件协同设计需同时优化器件布局、调度路径与电路拓扑，而非单纯追求纠缠资源保真度。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes can reduce the number of data qubits required per logical qubit, but their checks require long-range interactions on a planar device. We develop a singlet fabric for bivariate-bicycle codes on a silicon node network. Spin singlets are generated on shuttling lanes and distributed between nodes, where local operations consume them to measure non-local stabilizers. We construct two syndrome-extraction circuits, based on distributed GHZ states and ancilla teleportation, and evaluate them using hardware-constrained schedules and circuit-level memory simulations. We compare these circuits with CAbLECAR, which transports a mobile ancilla between the data qubits of each check. Teleportation can outperform CAbLECAR on its original layout when transport includes spin flips as well as dephasing. Placing the mobile ancilla on our node lattice, however, shortens the mean scheduled route for the [[144,12,12]] Gross code from 350 to 153 $μ$m and lowers its logical error rate up to 50-fold in the sampled bit-flip regime. Direct transport then achieves a lower logical error rate than either singlet circuit at every sampled Gross-code point. With frequent transport bit flips, the GHZ circuit overtakes direct transport once two-qubit gate and readout errors fall below about $4$-$5\times10^{-4}$, roughly half the value assumed here. Fixing the ancilla's data-visit order reveals a trade-off between shorter routes and the correlated errors produced by ancilla faults. Source heralding improves both singlet circuits, whereas phase purification helps the GHZ circuit only above a noise-dependent crossover and worsens teleportation throughout. These results show that the physical layout and interaction order can be as consequential as the choice of extraction circuit, and that cleaner entangled resources do not necessarily produce a more reliable logical memory.

Abstract: Although belief propagation (BP) is a widely adopted decoder for classical low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, its accuracy is limited in the quantum setting. At $p=10^{-3}$, \PriorTilt{} has lower mean decoding times than Relay-BP on all three codes and achieves mean decoding speedups of approximately $2.6\times$--$3.0\times$ over the beam search decoder. The visibility scales as $e^{-Γt_r}$ with the filter bandwidth $Γ$, and at long times the comb converges to the dressed-mode spectrum, which we also obtain analytically in quantitative agreement with exact diagonalization. As a practical application, quantum key distribution packets based on time-bin qubits and a BB84 protocol with a two-decoy-state method are successfully delivered to the three receivers achieving nonzero secret key rates. Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires decoders that combine accuracy, throughput, low latency, and scalability to large numbers of physical qubits. Across these benchmarks, PRISM matches the logical error rates of integer-programming-based decoders while being significantly faster, including at error rates up to 50% of threshold. We further optimize the decoder and its real-time hardware implementation to reduce latency while maintaining accuracy. Our optimized implementation on an AMD VP1902 FPGA can decode logical blocks of distance 8 in both space and time, demonstrating a latency of 6.59 microseconds and an amortized decoding time of 1.56 microseconds per logical block is possible. These results show that machine-learning decoding can provide accurate, low-latency inference for erasure-dominated fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

In addition, the gate complexity admits an instance-dependent bound $O(\log_2 M+ε)$, where $ε$ is an offset-dependent parameter defined in terms of binary Hamming weights. Abstract: Summers and Werner proved that Bell inequalities are violated maximally and generically in the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory in [S. J. Summers and R. Werner, Commun. Math. Phys. 110 (1987), 247-259]. Their criterion concerns two-party case, that is, there are two algebras of quantum observables: one algebra and its commutant. In this case, the maximal CHSH value is converged to in every normal state exactly when the algebra is strongly stable. For the tripartite Mermin functional we show that maximal violation in every normal state is equivalent to the existence of a common sequence of asymptotic GHZ stabilizers. Motivation: ADAPT-VQE与AVQITE等自适应变分算法在每次增长步都需筛选算符池，ADAPT-VQE还需重新优化全部参数，筛选与再优化构成主要开销。作者希望在变分虚时演化（VITE）框架下避开算符池与全局再优化，以降低线路与经典代价。

On five non-integrable systems (the mixed-field Ising chain and tori, and stretched H$_4$), this dynamic VITE reaches a relative error of $10^{-4}$ with 1.6-5.4 times fewer circuits than the cheapest of six to eight adaptive variants, including single-Pauli-string and qubit-excitation pools and batched ADAPT-VQE. On the spin models, a longitudinal-field scan shows that the advantage requires broken integrability, which makes adaptive screening expensive. Abstract: We introduce a continuous-variable version of twin-field quantum key distribution, using independent Gaussian-modulated coherent states and an untrusted single-photon relay. At fixed weak input-referred excess noise per arm, both rates per relay use scale as $\sqrtη$, where $η$ is the end-to-end transmissivity. For phase disclosure, we establish composable security against general coherent attacks using a bounded test of preparation intensities and sign-parity complementarity. For specified apparatus parameters, finite-key calculations incorporating detector inefficiency, dark counts, phase fluctuations, and reference overhead predict rates exceeding the pure-loss and matched thermal-loss repeaterless bounds. These results extend the twin-field advantage to Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum cryptography.

Abstract: Dynamical codes protect quantum information with sequences of measurements, generalizing the stabilizer formalism of static codes. However, the dynamical formalism has been developed exclusively for qubit systems. While Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes are promising candidates for quantum computing that encode qubits in the continuous phase space of oscillators, no dynamical formalism has been developed for them. In this work, we bridge this gap and introduce dynamical GKP codes, combining continuous-variable lattice encodings with time-dependent translation measurement dynamics. To obtain the update rules and the distance of a given measurement sequence, we first generalize GKP codes to subsystem GKP codes using scaled symplectic lattices. Building on this structure, we adapt the ancestry, detector, and benign-error machinery originally developed for dynamical qubit codes. From this construction, we obtain a general and efficient method to compute the syndrome and distance of a dynamical GKP code from a persistent stabilizer lattice and a bounded inference window. We illustrate the framework with the example of the two-mode square-hexagonal Floquet GKP code. This code has reduced measurement weight compared to both the square and hexagonal GKP codes, while combining their set of logical gates, resulting in easier implementation of the full single-qubit Clifford group. Finally, we show that measurements with irrational symplectic overlap, which have no analog in discrete qubit codes, reduce to destructive homodyne detection in the single-mode case. These results extend an important qubit-code tool to continuous-variable systems and identify a new bridge between discrete stabilizer measurements and continuous homodyne detection.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用QuSquare套件中的多体纠缠基准：在各处理器上制备GHZ态，用直接保真度估计（DFE）测量其保真度，并逐步增大比特数，寻找在统计误差范围内仍与真正多体纠缠（GME）阈值F=1/2相容的最大GHZ态规模N*。实验覆盖5家厂商共9台商用处理器（超导与离子阱两类技术），全部经由远程云服务访问，使用各厂商默认编译设置且不做误差缓解，以反映终端用户实际体验。此外，将基准结果与厂商公布的中位数校准数据比较，并对比不同统计判定规则（相容性判定 vs. 认证式GME判定）对结论的影响。

Result: 物理比特数与基准通过规模之间存在显著差距：最佳超导处理器仅达N*=27与N*=24比特，不足其寄存器规模的1/4；而12比特离子阱处理器以完整寄存器通过。中位数校准数据对基准结果预测能力很差。基准结论对统计判定规则高度敏感：若要求"认证的"GME，能在任一测试规模上成功的设备数由9台降至4台。

Abstract: Quantum computing has rapidly evolved into a diverse ecosystem of hardware platforms that differ in their underlying technologies and operational characteristics. Consequently, benchmarking has become essential for identifying the strengths of quantum processors, comparing their performance, and enabling informed decisions when selecting the most appropriate quantum computing platform for specific applications. In this work, we benchmark nine commercially accessible quantum processors from five vendors, spanning superconducting and trapped-ion technologies, using the multipartite entanglement benchmark from the QuSquare suite. All experiments were performed through remote cloud-access services, with the default compilation of each provider and without error mitigation, reflecting the performance experienced by end users. The benchmark estimates the fidelity of Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states by direct fidelity estimation and searches for the largest GHZ state whose fidelity is compatible, within the statistical error, with the genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) threshold $F=1/2$. Our results reveal a substantial gap between the total number of physical qubits available on a processor and the size of the largest GHZ state passing the benchmark: the best superconducting processors reach $N^*=27$ and $N^*=24$ qubits, less than a quarter of their registers, whereas the 12-qubit trapped-ion processor passes with its full register. We further show that median calibration data are poor predictors of the benchmark outcome, and that the reported sizes depend strongly on the statistical decision rule: requiring certified GME reduces the number of devices that succeed at any tested size from nine to four. These results provide a baseline for future cross-platform benchmarking of quantum processors.

Abstract: Quantum synchronization in open systems can arise from both coherent interactions and collective dissipation, yet their interplay remains poorly understood. Abstract: Information-flow security aims to prevent the leakage of confidential information through program execution. This problem becomes fundamentally subtler for quantum programs, because their states may involve superposition and entanglement, and their executions may involve intricate interactions between classical and quantum variables. This paper develops semantic foundations and verification methods for secure information flow in quantum programs. We formulate two notions of information-flow security. The first is a noninterference-based definition that naturally generalizes its classical counterpart. The second is a simulation-based definition that explicitly models an attacker, providing a clear operational interpretation of security. We prove that these two notions are equivalent.
  To verify the information-flow security of quantum programs, we develop a proof framework based on quantum Hoare logic. A key challenge is that the standard self-composition technique, which expresses information-flow security in Hoare logic by composing a program with a renamed copy of itself, fails in the quantum setting due to an obstruction analogous to the no-cloning theorem. To overcome this challenge, we introduce self-conjugation, a novel technique that leverages the deferred measurement principle and properties of unitary operations. We demonstrate our framework on examples drawn from the quantum one-time pad, quantum error correction, and anonymous entanglement generation.

</details>

Conclusion: 预测分歧带来实际代价：每个模型会把不同的化学系列送入实验评估。论文仅报告入选集合（入选者、标签及是否已被文献化学提及），不主张形式化的量子优势，也不解释两种向量为何产生不同分离。

Abstract: In drug discovery, hit identification selects a limited set of compounds for experimental evaluation from a larger collection of candidates. Because laboratory screening capacity is limited, that choice determines which chemical series continue into further investigation. A hit that is not assayed does not enter confirmation or the analog series, and that chemical series is not opened. For the DYRK1A kinase, the same test molecules were scored from a tabular representation of molecular descriptors and from a vector measured on a quantum processor, with the same RBF-SVM. The question is whether the two models propose the same set for experimental evaluation: they propose different compounds. The model on the vector measured on the quantum processor includes compounds that the tabular representation leaves out, and among them are already-labeled actives. The tabular representation includes actives that the vector measured on the quantum processor leaves out. What is reported is that set: who enters, with what label, and whether published chemistry already mentions it. No formal quantum advantage is claimed, nor a mechanism for why the two vectors separate differently. Those compounds fall within the neighborhood that both representations already cover; what changes is which model marks them active. From that follows the cost of predictive disagreement: each model would send a different chemical series to experimental evaluation.

### [76] [Quantum-Informed Graph Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14180)

TL;DR: 提出量子增强图分解(QEGD)启发式算法，以 QAOA 为子程序通过局部顶点合并来稀疏化图，在 MaxCut 上取得比 GDOA 更高的近似比，并在大多数测试图上以远低于 DC-QAOA 的耗时获得更高或相当的近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA 是近期量子组合优化算法，但其精度与资源需求随问题规模或结构变差，实用性受限。GDOA 通过迭代缩减问题规模至 QAOA 可解范围来解决此问题，但需要求解变量数呈指数级的整数规划，且因整数规划含松弛项而可能得到次优解。

Method: 提出 QEGD 启发式算法，其新颖点是将 QAOA 用作子程序，通过局部顶点合并(vertex-merging)来稀疏化图；先用 QEGD 对 MaxCut 问题稀疏化，再用 QAOA 求解。随后在随机 5-正则、6-正则图及 Erdős–Rényi 图上与 GDOA、QAOA^2、ParaQAOA、DC-QAOA 比较近似比与求解时间。

Result: 在所有测试的随机 5-正则与 6-正则图上，QEGD+QAOA 的近似比均高于 GDOA；在 Erdős–Rényi 图上，平均近似比高于 QAOA^2 与 ParaQAOA，在稀疏图上高于 DC-QAOA，在较稠密图上与 DC-QAOA 相当，但平均实际耗时仅为 DC-QAOA 的 0.4%–5%。

Conclusion: 以 QAOA 作为图稀疏化子程序的 QEGD 在近似质量上优于 GDOA 及多种 QAOA 变体，同时在时间效率上显著优于 DC-QAOA，说明"分解+子程序稀疏化"是提升 QAOA 实用性的有效途径。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a near-term quantum algorithm for solving combinatorial optimization problems, but its accuracy and resource requirements may scale poorly with problem size or structure, reducing its utility in practice. Approaches such as the Graph Decomposition for Optimization Algorithm (GDOA) seek to address this by iteratively reducing an input problem until it reaches a size where it can be solved using QAOA while maintaining the same optimal solution. This algorithm, however, requires solving integer programs that have exponentially many variables, and can result in suboptimal solutions, as the integer programs may contain slack terms. In this work, we propose a heuristic called the Quantum Enhanced Graph Decomposition (QEGD) algorithm. Its novelty lies in its use of QAOA as a subroutine for sparsifying a graph through local vertex-merging. The algorithm, when used to sparsify MaxCut problems that are then solved using QAOA, achieves higher approximation ratios across all tested graphs than GDOA for random 5-regular and 6-regular graphs. We then compare the time-to-solution and approximation ratio of QEGD to other QAOA variants a collection of Erdös-Rényi graphs. QEGD has higher approximation ratios on average than QAOA$^2$ and ParaQAOA on all tested graphs. It also results in higher average approximation ratios than DC-QAOA on sparse Erdös-Rényi graphs and comparable average approximation ratio on denser Erdös-Rényi graphs. However, its average wall-clock time is between 0.4\% and 5\% of the average DC-QAOA wall-clock time.

</details>

*Gabriel Maresch,Erik Schulze,Jnan Yalla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子辅助反事实遗憾最小化（QA-CFR）框架，利用量子振幅估计降低有界收益不完全信息博弈中遗憾更新的计算复杂度，保持经典CFR的收敛性，并给出可利用度界、NISQ兼容变体及资源需求分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分级奖励结构的不完全信息博弈中，均衡计算需在指数级策略空间上估计收益期望，计算代价极高。CFR虽能有效逼近Nash均衡，但其蒙特卡洛变体在从采样博弈树轨迹估计反事实值时产生大量采样开销。

Method: 提出QA-CFR框架：以量子振幅估计（QAE）替代采样来估计反事实值，从而降低遗憾更新的计算复杂度；理论上推导其可利用度（exploitability）上界，并讨论NISQ兼容的振幅估计变体及其资源需求结构。

Result: 给出QA-CFR的可利用度理论上界，并保持与经典CFR对应版本的收敛性；分析了NISQ兼容振幅估计变体的结构与资源需求。

Conclusion: QA-CFR在有界收益不完全信息博弈中具备降低遗憾更新复杂度的理论潜力，但其经验性优势的验证路径（实验设计与可行性）仍有待后续工作完成。

Abstract: Imperfect-information games with a graded-reward structure present significant computational challenges for equilibrium computation, since the payoff expectations must be estimated across exponentially large strategy spaces. Counterfactual Regret Minimization over the years has proven effective for approximating Nash equilibria, but Monte Carlo variants create a large overhead of sampling costs when the counterfactual values are estimated from sampled game-tree trajectories. We propose a novel Quantum-Assisted Counterfactual Regret Minimization, a framework that uses Quantum Amplitude Estimation to reduce the computational complexity of the regret updates in bounded-payoff imperfect-information games, while preserving the convergence of its classical counterpart. We give a theoretical analysis of the resulting exploitability bound, discuss NISQ-compatible amplitude-estimation variants, the structure of their resource requirements, and outline what is needed to validate the potential advantage empirically.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a nonasymptotic electric-flux truncation bound for the entire vacuum-relative low-energy window of the original pure compact $U(1)$ lattice Hamiltonian. It controls finite-time hard-wall evolution from the normalized projection of the same initial state. An analytic finite-volume strong-coupling example in this model certifies a nonvacuum band and an order-one plaquette signal change preserved by a specified finite cutoff. The same estimate yields precision-dependent sufficient cutoffs, an alternative linear isometric encoding by polar decomposition for reference-entangled inputs, and local electric-square readout bounds. At fixed positive bare coupling, physical volume, window width, and duration, cutoffs scaling as $β\log N/\log\log N$ with $β>2$ make state and bounded-readout truncation errors vanish under lattice refinement.

### [79] [Classical phase unwrapping enables large-time-step unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14214)
*Tushar Kumar Jain,Rahul Maitra,Nicholas J. Mayhall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出经典后处理框架CURE-QKSD，利用大时间步下场外相位一致性景观解缠绕，恢复被2π/τ混叠的本征能量，使UQKSD可用更小、条件数更好的Krylov基。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: CURE-QKSD（Classical Unwrapping for Resolving Eigenvalues）为纯经典后处理：不改动UQKSD电路与广义本征值问题，仅基于一次（必要时两次）UQKSD运行的相位一致性景观（phase-consistency landscape）恢复缺失的2π/τ整数倍。

Conclusion: CURE-QKSD使混叠时间步对UQKSD可用，从而可采用更小、条件数更优的Krylov基进行分子能量计算。

Abstract: For computing molecular energies on early fault-tolerant hardware, unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization (UQKSD) avoids both the deep circuits of quantum phase estimation and the Pauli-term sampling of variational methods by extracting the energies from a projected real-time propagator. At a small time step $τ$, nearly linearly dependent Krylov vectors make the overlap matrix ill-conditioned. Our tests at fixed total evolution time show that additional Krylov vectors then add no accuracy, while the thresholded energy becomes increasingly sensitive to the choice of cutoff. A larger time step improves the conditioning and reduces the Krylov dimension $M$ needed for a target accuracy, but the energies are then known only modulo $2π/τ$. We present Classical Unwrapping for Resolving Eigenvalues (CURE-QKSD), a classical post-processing method that leaves the UQKSD circuits and generalized eigenvalue problem unchanged and recovers the missing multiples of $2π/τ$ from phase-consistency landscapes of one or, when needed, two UQKSD runs. In noiseless tests on six molecules with 12--20 qubits, all 28 targeted energies are recovered in their aliased regimes with final errors of at most $1.1\times10^{-7}$~Ha. Under Gaussian noise with $σ=10^{-4}$, the median error of the ground or first excited state reaches chemical accuracy at large aliased time steps with $M=20$--$65$, whereas uncorrected principal-branch energies are off by $0.7$--$115$~Ha. CURE-QKSD therefore makes aliased time steps usable for UQKSD calculations with smaller, better-conditioned Krylov bases.

</details>


### [80] [Reading long-range information from a separable quantum record requires entangled measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14295)
*Qingtian Miao*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨"局域产生、不含纠缠的记录"能否承载长程信息，以及这种信息的可读出性是否依赖于测量资源（可分测量 vs. 纠缠测量），从而厘清记录中的纠缠与测量中纠缠在信息提取中的作用差异。

Method: 设置初始无关联的Ising自旋对，用量子比特探针对每个近邻键进行局域相互作用编码，探针两态重叠为1-p，所得记录完全可分。接收方获得一份记录副本，判断两个远距离自旋同向或反向。在方格晶格上分析两类读出：可分测量效应（含LOCC及跨探针任意割部分转置保持正定的测量）；以及联合（纠缠）读出，比较二者成功率随自旋间距的变化。

Result: 对0<p≤p0=1-1/√2，最佳可分测量读出的成功率随自旋间距增大趋近1/2，对上述所有LOCC及PPT类测量均成立。对p*=0.29181≤p≤1，联合读出使成功率高出1/2一个与距离无关的正量。区间[p*,p0]给出两类读出之间的严格分离。

Conclusion: 局域产生且无纠缠的记录仍可携带长程信息，但该信息只能通过具有纠缠效应的测量读出；可分测量在足够大的距离上无法提取，表明纠缠测量效应是读取此类非纠缠记录中长程关联的必要资源。

Abstract: Qubit probes record the relative orientation of neighboring, initially uncorrelated Ising spins through local interactions. Each probe encodes one bond label in two states with overlap $1-p$. The resulting probe record is fully separable. A receiver gets one copy and guesses whether two distant spins point in the same or opposite directions. On the square lattice, the best readout with separable measurement effects approaches success probability $1/2$ as the spin separation grows, for $0<p\le p_0=1-1/\sqrt2$. This includes all local operations with classical communication. The same conclusion holds for every measurement whose effects remain positive under partial transposition across every cut of the probes. For $p_*=0.29181\le p\le1$, a joint readout of the same record maintains a success probability above $1/2$ by a positive amount independent of distance. The overlap $[p_*,p_0]$ gives a strict separation between the readouts. A record that is created locally and contains no entanglement can therefore hold long-range information that only measurements with entangled effects can read.

Motivation: 传统c面4H-SiC的几何构型限制了共振光激发与实验方案，需要更灵活的晶面以从晶圆表面对色心进行共振光学访问；同时量子网络要求不同发射体光谱不可区分。

Method: 采用a面4H-SiC晶圆，以可扩展CMOS工艺在横向pin二极管本征区集成V2（立方格位）硅空位；测量零偏下光跃迁线宽；利用Stark效应在低温下原位测量局域电场，并与宏观TCAD仿真对比；通过反向偏压Stark效应调节单个发射体的ZPL。

Result: 经完整半导体工艺后色心仍保持良好光学质量，零偏下线宽低于80 MHz；量子传感器揭示出高度局域化的场拥挤现象；Stark调谐补偿了不同发射体的光谱差异，在瞬态电荷涨落弛豫后实现超过四小时的光谱稳定性。

Conclusion: a面4H-SiC是面向未来干涉实验与量子光子集成电路的稳健平台。

Abstract: Silicon carbide has emerged as a highly promising platform for scalable quantum technologies, with the silicon vacancy serving as a robust spin qubit and quantum sensor. However, conventional c-plane 4H-SiC restricts the experimental scheme and resonant optical excitation to the sample's geometry. The usage of a-plane substrates enables more versatile resonant optical access to the color center through the wafer surface. Here, we demonstrate the electrical control and optical addressability of $V_{2}$ (cubic lattice site) silicon vacancies within the intrinsic region of lateral pin-diodes fabricated on a-plane 4H-SiC wafers using a scalable complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) process. Despite extensive semiconductor processing, the integrated color centers exhibit good optical quality, evidenced by narrow optical transition linewidths below 80 MHz at zero bias. These embedded quantum sensors are utilized for in situ measurement of local electric fields at cryogenic temperatures via the Stark effect. Comparisons with macroscopic technology computer-aided design simulations reveal the value of these integrated quantum sensors to help understand cryogenic device behavior, for example in predicting highly localized field crowding. Furthermore, to address the spectral indistinguishability required for quantum networks, we precisely tune the zero-phonon line (ZPL) of individual emitters via the reverse-bias Stark effect. This successfully compensates for the spectral variations of distinct emitters. Following the relaxation of transient charge fluctuations, the Stark-tuned emitters exhibit spectral stability over measurement periods exceeding four hours. Our results establish a-plane 4H-SiC as a robust platform for future interference experiments and quantum photonic integrated circuits.

</details>


### [82] [Error Resilience and Unitary 2-Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14314)
*Zheng-Hang Sun,Fabian Ballar Trigueros,Xhek Turkeshi,Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示编码器偏离Haar二阶矩统计（即非unitary 2-design）会破坏编码-错误-解码电路的纠错相，并给出将恢复保真度与二阶幺正框架势联系起来的普适不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明，当编码酉算子取自unitary 2-design系综时，编码-错误-解码电路存在低于阈值误差强度即保真度趋于1的纠错相。但实际物理体系（如量子多体系统）的编码器通常只是近似2-design，偏离程度如何影响纠错相缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 推导恢复保真度与系综二阶幺正框架势（frame potential，度量系综接近unitary 2-design程度）之间的普适不等式；以掺杂可调数量非高斯门的matchgate电路为范例，解析分析掺杂量对纠错相的影响；并将同一机制推广至含实验动机的全连接自旋模型等哈密顿量编码器。

Result: 解析证明未掺杂matchgate电路在热力学极限下不存在稳定的纠错相；随非高斯门掺杂增加，编码系综趋近近似unitary 2-design，纠错保护机制得以恢复。哈密顿量编码器（含全连接自旋模型）遵循相同机制。

Conclusion: 恢复保真度可作为诊断量子多体系统偏离unitary 2-design行为的指标，框架势不等式为编码电路纠错能力提供了定量判据。

Abstract: Encoding-error-decoding circuits provide a setting for studying protection of quantum information against errors. In these circuits, an encoding unitary is followed by an error layer, decoding by the inverse unitary, and ancilla postselection. For encoders drawn from unitary 2-design ensembles, these circuits exhibit an error-protecting phase below a threshold error strength in which the fidelity between the input and recovered logical states approaches unity. Here, we show how departures from Haar second-moment statistics of the encoder can destabilize the error-protecting phase and derive a universal inequality linking recovery fidelity to the second unitary frame potential, which quantifies the ensemble's proximity to a unitary 2-design. This connection is exemplified by matchgate circuits doped with a tunable number of non-Gaussian gates. We analytically show that undoped circuits lack a stable error-protecting phase in the thermodynamic limit and that increasing the doping restores the error protection mechanism as the encoder ensemble approaches an approximate unitary 2-design. Further examples of Hamiltonian encoders, including an experimentally motivated all-to-all spin model, follow the same mechanism. Our results identify recovery fidelity as a diagnostic of departures from unitary 2-design behavior in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [83] [Dynamical Casimir emission from Floquet-driven Landau polaritons: Gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14339)
*S. Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了周期驱动Landau极化激元的精确理论，证明单纯调制磁场并不能实现Floquet超辐射相变（SRPT），而只会产生动力学卡西米尔效应（Faraday驱动的极化激元–磁等离激元对与双模压缩光子对）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有预测认为仅调制磁场即可增强周期平均耦合并诱导Floquet超辐射相变；但线性系统的超强耦合由玻色Hopfield模型描述，规范不变性禁止SRPT，因此需要严格检验该预测是否成立。

Method: 发展周期驱动Landau极化激元的精确理论：计入磁通调制感生的电场（法拉第定律），借助Kohn定理将问题严格约化为电子质心模与腔模的耦合，其哈密顿量在每一时刻均为平方和；用Krein定理证明高于可计算的Krein频率时系统全阶稳定（给出自足证明）；推导广义Thomas–Reiche–Kuhn求和规则，并证明排除Floquet光子凝聚的规范定理。

Result: Krein频率以上系统稳定；其下仅在共振舌区不稳定，对应真空产生粒子对（动力学卡西米尔效应）。高频驱动下电子感受到的是平均场加有质动力势阱（Fock–Darwin问题），而非增强耦合。所预言的相变源于在固定Landau能级基下对耦合取平均，所得有效模型违反TRK求和规则。规范不变性要求必须调制并对最小耦合哈密顿量本身（而非其投影参数）取平均。磁调制实际产生低阈值Faraday驱动的极化激元–磁等离激元对，以及双模压缩达5–10 dB的双色光子对。

Conclusion: 磁场调制不产生SRPT，而是产生动力学卡西米尔辐射；该"调制并平均最小耦合哈密顿量而非其投影参数"的规则适用于任何线性、最小耦合系统，从而纠正了基于固定Landau能级基平均的Floquet SRPT预言。

Abstract: Ultrastrong light--matter coupling of linear systems, such as Landau polaritons formed by a two-dimensional electron gas in a terahertz cavity, is described by the bosonic Hopfield model, and gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition (SRPT). Modulating the magnetic field alone has been predicted to enhance the cycle-averaged coupling and induce a Floquet SRPT. We develop an exact theory of periodically driven Landau polaritons. Including the electric field induced by the modulated flux (Faraday's law), the problem reduces exactly, by Kohn's theorem, to the electronic center-of-mass mode coupled to the cavity, whose Hamiltonian is a sum of squares at every instant. A theorem of Krein then guarantees stability to all orders above a computable drive frequency, the Krein frequency, for which we give a self-contained proof. Below it, the system is stable except within resonance tongues where the drive creates pairs from the vacuum, the dynamical Casimir effect. At high drive frequency the electrons experience the mean field plus a ponderomotive trap (the Fock--Darwin problem), not an enhanced coupling. A gauge theorem excludes a Floquet photon condensate for any band structure, including Kane nonparabolicity, for which we derive a generalized Thomas--Reiche--Kuhn (TRK) sum rule. The predicted transition arises from averaging the coupling in a fixed Landau-level basis, which yields an effective model that violates the TRK sum rule; under driving, gauge invariance also requires the induced Faraday field. The rule to modulate and average the minimal-coupling Hamiltonian, not the parameters of its projection, applies to any linear, minimally coupled system. Instead of an SRPT, magnetic modulation produces dynamical Casimir emission: Faraday-driven polariton--magnetoplasmon pairs at low threshold, and two-color photon pairs with up to 5--10~dB of two-mode squeezing.

</details>


### [84] [High-Speed Control of a Fluxonium Qubit via Picosecond Pulses from Josephson Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14358)
*M. V. Bastrakova,D. S. Pashin,V. A. Vozhakov,N. V. Klenov,I. I. Soloviev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约瑟夫森数字电路产生的皮秒级单磁通量子(SFQ)脉冲的fluxonium量子比特控制策略，通过两种互补协议实现高保真单比特门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原为transmon设计的SFQ控制协议在低频fluxonium上效率低下：磁通与电荷耦合的不对称性导致要么泄漏过大、要么门时间过长，需要新的控制方案。

Method: 提出两种互补协议：(1)通过磁通通道施加高频SFQ脉冲包，解析清晰、鲁棒性强；(2)结合相位偏移的磁通与电荷脉冲序列，增强共振驱动并缩短门时间。采用多能级仿真评估。

Result: 单比特门保真度超过99.99%；简单协议在量子比特参数与磁通偏置适度变化下保持鲁棒；组合协议在保持高保真的同时显著加速门操作。

Conclusion: 基于SFQ的低温控制是实现fluxonium量子比特快速、精确、可扩展操控的现实可行路径。

Abstract: We develop a control strategy for fluxonium qubits based on picosecond single-flux-quantum (SFQ) pulses generated by Josephson digital circuits. A major challenge is that SFQ protocols originally designed for transmon control become inefficient for low-frequency fluxonium. In this regime, the asymmetry of flux and charge couplings leads either to excessive leakage or excessively long gate times. We show that this limitation can be overcome by two complementary protocols. The first protocol employs high-frequency SFQ pulse packets applied through the flux channel, offering a simple, analytically transparent, and robust approach for high-fidelity single-qubit control. The second protocol combines phase-shifted flux and charge pulse sequences to enhance the resonant drive and reduce the gate duration. Multilevel simulations demonstrate single-qubit gate fidelities above $99.99\%$; the simpler protocol remains robust under moderate variations of qubit parameters and flux bias, while the combined protocol preserves high fidelity while substantially accelerating the gate. These results establish SFQ-based cryogenic control as a realistic route to fast, accurate, and scalable manipulation of fluxonium qubits.

</details>


### [85] [Invariance can prevent overfitting and shortcut learning in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14368)
*Giuseppe D'Ambruoso,Giovanni Gramegna,Domenico Pomarico,Giuseppe Magnifico,Cosmo Lupo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在编码、线路结构、可训练参数量与动力学李代数完全受控的条件下，比较等变（旋转/反射不变）与非不变量子电路的差别，发现不变性带来更好的泛化、抗捷径学习与抗噪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变量子神经网络按设计内嵌对称性，但以往研究中等变模型与非等变模型往往在门集、结构或规模上也不同，难以单独剥离"对称性"本身的贡献。作者希望在受控实验中单独考察不变性的作用。

Method: 构造标签在二面体群 D4 下不变的图像分类任务；设计四个八比特模型，其编码方式、线路结构、可训练参数数目以及动力学李代数均相同，仅"是否不变"不同，进行不变型与非不变型的对照。指标包括泛化误差、对捷径学习的鲁棒性，以及在真实噪声模型下的表现。任务为四个二分类任务，含 MNIST 及星系、红细胞两个科学数据集。

Result: 在仅用数十张图像训练的情况下，不变模型在四个二分类任务上泛化优于非不变模型。不变性还使模型免于"朝向捷径"：当训练数据带类别相关的角标水印时，非不变模型会学到水印，水印位置改变后准确率常跌至随机水平，而不变模型不受影响。在真实噪声模型下，不变模型的准确率优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 在严格控制混杂因素的条件下，对称性（不变性）本身即为量子图像分类器带来可分离的收益：更好的小样本泛化、对捷径学习的免疫性以及噪声鲁棒性，说明等变设计是值得采用的原则性归纳偏置。

Abstract: Many image classification tasks have labels that are unchanged by rotations and reflections. Equivariant quantum neural networks incorporate such symmetries by design, but the specific benefit of symmetry is difficult to isolate. Equivariant quantum circuits are usually compared with architectures that also differ in gates, structure or size. Here, we investigate the role of invariance in a controlled setting, classifying images whose labels are invariant under the dihedral group $D_4$. We compare four eight-qubit models with identical encoding, circuit structure and number of trainable parameters, and equivalent dynamical Lie algebras, and benchmark invariant implementations against non-invariant ones in terms of generalization error, robustness to shortcut learning and resilience to a realistic noise model. On four binary tasks, including MNIST and two scientific data sets of galaxies and red blood cells, the invariant models trained on a few tens of images show better generalization than non-invariant ones. Invariance also makes the models immune to orientation shortcuts: when trained with a class-dependent corner watermark, the non-invariant models learn the watermark and often drop to chance level when it is moved. We show that the gain in accuracy of invariant models persists also under a realistic noise model.

</details>


### [86] [Operational limits of reference renewal for retained quantum-service results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14376)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究通过"引用续期"(reference renewal)协议重检已保留的物理量子服务结果：12个转换各保留6条结果，10个完成续期，8个达到预设终点，5个仅凭保留结果即成功；精确单侧p=0.0546875不足以支持恢复假设，且暴露引用不完整、绑定失败与串行等待等操作限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估被保留的历史量子服务结果在预设协议下能否被"续期"式恢复，并检验续期相对离线重建与回溯式"绑定+oracle"校验是否带来额外的方法学或数值正确性收益。

Method: 预注册协议下的引用续期实验：12个转换各保留6条结果；离线方案从保留计数重建判定；对照方案为回溯性"绑定+oracle"检查及由精确oracle提供校准分数的变体；采用精确单侧检验。

Result: 10/12完成续期，8个达预设终点，5个仅用保留结果成功；精确单侧p=0.0546875未达显著；回溯绑定+oracle在不续期情况下通过全部60条可用转换结果；引入oracle校准分数后未显示续期的增量数值正确性效用。

Conclusion: 该研究未能证明"恢复假设"，也未证明续期在数值正确性上具有增量价值；引用不完整、绑定失败与串行等待构成主要操作瓶颈，离线重建与回溯校验可替代续期完成判定复现。

Abstract: We implement reference renewal to reconsider retained physical quantum-service results under a prespecified protocol. Twelve transitions retained six results each; ten completed renewal, eight met the prespecified endpoint, and five succeeded using retained results alone. The exact one-sided p=0.0546875 does not establish the recovery hypothesis. Incomplete references, binding failures and sequential waits expose operational limits. Offline reconstruction recovers decisions from preserved counts. Retrospective binding-plus-oracle checking accepts all 60 usable-transition results without renewal. With the exact oracle also supplying calibration scores, the study does not demonstrate incremental numerical-correctness utility of renewal.

</details>


### [87] [Implementing rapid reset of superconducting qubits with on-chip dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14391)
*Feiyu Li,Yang Liu,Zhen Yang,Zhenxing Liu,Jiayu Ding,Yang Gao,Xuehui Liang,Weijie Sun,Yanjun Ma,Chuhong Yang,Guangming Xue,He Wang,Fei Yan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速高保真比特复位是量子态初始化与量子纠错的前提。传统超导比特复位依赖连接外部电路的耗散元件，使比特暴露于高温环境的热噪声中，且难以扩展。

Method: 采用由谐振器与片上电阻负载耦合构成的专用耗散单元，对多个transmon比特进行复位与泄漏消除；该结构在提供Purcell保护的同时将比特与较热的外部电路隔离。

Result: |g>-|e>跃迁的中位复位时间常数约48 ns，有效浴温30 mK；400 ns复位脉冲后，初始化为|g>和|e>的比特残余激发态布居分别为0.29%和0.67%；|f>态泄漏残余布居降至0.51%。

Conclusion: 该方案简单、可复现、易集成，为超导量子处理器中的耗散工程提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Rapid and high-fidelity qubit reset is essential for quantum state initialization and quantum error correction. Conventional reset protocols for superconducting qubits often rely on dissipative elements connected to external circuitry, exposing qubits to thermal noise from higher-temperature environments. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a scalable approach to qubit reset and leakage removal over multiple transmon qubits using dedicated dissipation units comprising resonators coupled to on-chip resistive loads. This architecture provides Purcell protection while isolating the qubits from warmer external circuitry. We achieve a median reset time constant of approximately 48 ns for the $|g\rangle-|e\rangle$ transition, with an effective bath temperature of 30 mK. A 400-ns reset pulse yields residual excited-state populations of $0.29\%$ and $0.67\%$ for qubits initialized in $|g\rangle$ and $|e\rangle$, respectively. We further demonstrate efficient leakage removal from the $|f\rangle$ state, reducing its residual population to $0.51\%$. These results establish a simple, reproducible, and easy-to-integrate approach to dissipation engineering in superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [88] [Simulating closed- and open-system dynamics of Tavis--Cummings lattices on fault-tolerant quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14398)
*Soumya Sarkar,Rakshit Gharat,Nam Nguyen,Gavin Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析含原子分辨的Tavis–Cummings 晶格在容错量子计算下闭/开系统模拟的逻辑资源，提出基于梯子算符块编码的原位更新跃迁线路并给出总体资源估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类晶格含多发射体腔，实时关联在二维情形下经典计算代价高，尤其当不等发射体弛豫或退相干速率破坏置换对称性时；同时缺乏含态制备与测量开销的容错资源参考。

Method: 基于 ladder-operator 块编码构造光子跃迁与局域光–物质交换线路并组合成晶格哈密顿量；通过原位更新源/目标光子占据去除双寄存器实现中的 L⌈log2(n_max+1)⌉ 辅助光子寄存器；闭系统用广义量子信号处理做实时演化并界定延迟腔谱函数估计成本；开系统（光子损耗、发射体弛豫与退相）用采样的 first-order dilation 并界定瞬态光子统计（如 g^(2)(0)）所需运行次数与时间步。

Result: 在 16–256 个腔、均匀系数的三个算例中，匹配共享制备、精度、控制与工作区约定下，受控行走的 Toffoli 门数比成对双寄存器实现少 4.75%–8.11%；并给出闭/开系统目标量估计的量子线路与时间步资源界。

Conclusion: 原位光子寄存器更新的跃迁线路可稳定降低 Toffoli 开销，配合 GQSP 与采样一阶 dilation 的资源界，为容错光–物质晶格模拟成本提供显式参考基准。

Abstract: Tavis--Cummings lattices couple cavities containing several emitters and host polaritonic many-body dynamics whose real-time correlations are costly to compute classically in two dimensions, especially when unequal emitter relaxation or dephasing rates break permutation symmetry. We analyze logical resources for fault-tolerant closed- and open-system simulation of an atom-resolved lattice with $L$ cavities, $N$ emitters per cavity, and photon cutoff $n\_{\max}$, including target observable estimation. Building on ladder-operator block encoding, we construct circuits for photon hopping and local light--matter exchange and combine them to encode the lattice Hamiltonian. By updating source and destination photon occupations in place, the hopping circuit eliminates the auxiliary $L\lceil\log_2(n\_{\max}+1)\rceil$-qubit photon register used by two-register implementations. Across three uniform-coefficient examples with $16$ to $256$ cavities, the controlled walk uses $4.75$--$8.11%$ fewer Toffoli gates than the paired two-register implementation under matched shared preparation, precision, control, and workspace conventions. For closed systems, we implement real-time evolution with generalized quantum signal processing and bound the cost of estimating retarded cavity spectral functions. For open systems with photon loss, emitter relaxation, and dephasing, we use a sampled first-order dilation and bound the runs and timesteps needed to estimate transient photon statistics such as $g^{(2)}(0)$. With state-preparation and measurement costs explicit, these estimates provide a reference for assessing fault-tolerant light--matter lattice simulation costs.

</details>


### [89] [Multidimensional Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14406)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典turbo迭代译码思想推广到多维量子分组turbo码，提出用于多层乘积码的turbo译码理论，给出算法并在去极化信道上仿真了三维情形性能。

Conclusion: 多维量子分组turbo码可将turbo迭代译码有效扩展至多层乘积码，三维情形在去极化信道上的仿真验证了该理论的可行性。

Abstract: The theory of turbo iterative decoding was brought into the quantum coding framework through the development of quantum serial turbo codes and quantum block turbo codes. In this paper, we present a theory for multidimensional quantum block turbo codes, a turbo decoding technique for multi layer product codes. We describe its algorithm and simulate the performance for the 3D case on a depolarizing channel.

</details>


### [90] [The energetic cost of loading classical data in quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14422)
*Joao F. Doriguello,Alessandro Luongo,Harshit Verma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子数据加载视为硬件过程，量化超导处理器上QRAM查询的动态能耗，在12比特桶旅式QRAM上得到0.969的查询保真度、3.51e-4 J动态能量、4.38 W平均功率（峰值64.4 W）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习、化学与密码分析中的量子算法通常假设对经典数据的相干访问几乎没有物理代价，忽视了数据加载的硬件能耗成本，本文试图补上这一空白。

Method: 采用metric-noise-resource框架，将查询保真度与功率关联到门持续时间、相对双比特驱动强度以及多级制冷机中的衰减分布，并计入热光子与门误差；分析线性深度多路复用器与抗噪声的桶旅（bucket-brigade）架构，将后者适配到超导原生门集，并用完全分解的门调度细化其保真度分析。

Conclusion: QRAM查询的能耗不可忽略，其功率强烈依赖工作点与制冷机衰减设计，硬件层面的能效优化应成为量子算法可行性评估的一部分。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for machine learning, chemistry, and cryptanalysis often assume coherent access to classical data at negligible physical cost. We instead treat data loading as a hardware process and quantify the dynamic energetic cost of a quantum random access memory (QRAM) query on a superconducting processor. Using the metric-noise-resource framework, we relate query fidelity and power to gate duration, relative two-qubit drive strength, and the distribution of attenuation across a multi-stage refrigerator, while accounting for thermal photons and gate errors. We analyze the linear-depth multiplexer and the noise-resilient bucket-brigade architectures, adapting the latter to a native superconducting gate set and refining its fidelity analysis using the fully decomposed gate schedule. For a representative bucket-brigade QRAM query with a $12$-qubit address register we obtain a query fidelity of $0.969$. The query consumes $3.51\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{J}$ of dynamic energy, corresponding to an average dynamic power of $4.38\,\mathrm{W}$ and a peak of $64.4\,\mathrm{W}$. Our optimization shows that, at fixed target fidelity, the operating point and attenuation profile can change the average power by a factor of two or more.

</details>

Abstract: Thermal amplification enables sensitive control of heat currents, providing a basis for heat management and thermal signal processing in quantum devices. For a harmonic oscillator coupled to emitter, collector, and base thermal reservoirs, we recover the absence of thermal amplification when system-reservoir interactions permit only single-quantum exchange. A controlled weak-nonlinearity expansion, supported by numerical stationary solutions, shows that two-quantum exchange at the emitter enables amplification and predicts its onset. When two-quantum exchange dominates at the emitter and collector, varying the base temperature redistributes populations between even and odd oscillator sectors. This changes the emitter and collector heat currents far more than the base current, enabling large thermal amplification. We propose a realization using a longitudinally coupled auxiliary two-level system and bath spectral filtering, specifying conditions for its elimination to yield the required oscillator dynamics. These results establish nonlinear system-reservoir coupling as a resource for controlling heat flow and enabling thermal amplification.

</details>


### [92] [Finite-Key Readiness and Authentication-Key Replenishment in QKD-Assisted UAV Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14461)
*Dang H. Le,Ban Q. Tran,Susan Mengel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量化无人机为地面网关提供基于有限密钥BB84的新鲜量子密钥时，密钥释放等待/拒绝对受保护任务完成时间与悬停能耗的代价，并给出脉冲预算窗口与截止期限下的可行损耗边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量速率模型无法刻画"密钥必须先成功获取才能开始受保护计算"这一依赖，也无法反映块大小受截止期限约束的限制；需要量化该耦合带来的时延与能耗代价。

Method: 构建计数级蒙特卡洛模型（并由半解析模型复现）：可信静止无人机对两个地面网关各进行一次参数预设的有限密钥诱骗态BB84，将密钥接受与否联动至受保护的上行、计算、下行流程，计入失败尝试与悬停能耗；以代理基准的近零成本认证哈希为参照，并设高成本压力参考。

Result: 26 dB有效光损耗下，新鲜密钥使两任务第95百分位完成时间由10.37 s升至28.65 s，悬停能耗同比例增长；标量速率模型无法给出块大小的截止约束。脉冲预算窗口下界由认证密钥补充决定、上界由截止期限决定；认证扣减移动下界，哈希成本不影响。35 s截止下，非空窗口的最大损耗为1%失准27 dB、2%失准22 dB，3%失准无可行配置；在假定获取概率下，26 dB及18–20 dB（2%失准）配置在留出试验上的同时下置信界为96.9–97.3%；高成本压力参考将损耗限降至26/20 dB。

Conclusion: 密钥获取的不确定性可与截止期限概率定量挂钩；误码率或损耗的小幅上升会使截止概率远低于目标并耗尽认证密钥，而协议安全参数不变，说明期限约束由认证密钥补充与时间预算而非安全性决定。

Abstract: A service that needs fresh quantum keys must wait for key release, and may be denied it, before protected computation starts. We quantify this cost for a trusted stationary UAV serving two ground gateways, each obtaining its keys from one finite-key decoy-state BB84 attempt with parameters fixed in advance. A count-level Monte Carlo model, reproduced by a semi-analytic model, links key acceptance to protected upload, computation, and download, including failed attempts and hover energy. At 26~dB effective optical loss, with authentication hashing at its proxy-benchmarked, near-zero cost, fresh keys raise the 95th-percentile completion time of both tasks from 10.37 to 28.65~s; hover energy grows in proportion. The scalar rate models evaluated here miss the deadline limit on the block size. The pulse budget lies in a window bounded below by authentication-key replenishment and above by the deadline; for a fixed source, channel, and acceptance model, the authentication debit shifts the floor, whereas hashing cost does not. At a 35-s deadline the largest tested loss with a non-empty window is 27~dB at 1\% misalignment and 22~dB at 2\%; at 3\% no tested setting has one. Conditional on the assumed acquisition probability, the selected configurations meet this deadline with simultaneous lower confidence bounds of 96.9--97.3\% on held-out trials, at 26~dB and at 18--20~dB with 2\% misalignment. A high-cost stress reference that charges hashing in full narrows the window and lowers these loss limits to 26 and 20~dB at the reference processing capacity. A small rise in error rate or loss pushes the deadline probability well below its target and drains authentication keys, while the protocol's security parameter is unchanged.

</details>


### [93] [Nonperturbative string dynamics in a (2+1)D lattice gauge theory on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14484)
*Sanziana Stelea,Anthony Gandon,Francesco Tacchino,Jad C. Halimeh,Ivano Tavernelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用63个超导量子比特（含12个辅助比特）量子模拟(2+1)维Z₂格点规范场论，在强禁闭与非微扰解禁闭区间研究初始局域弦的演化，借助规范冗余实现误差检测，可测至15个二阶Trotter步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电通量弦是规范场论中禁闭的核心机制；二维空间中弦的演化涉及电荷运动、对产生与横向形变等竞争过程。经典方法难以处理超出微扰极限的动力学，而纠缠增长限制了张量网络可模拟的时间尺度，因此亟需量子模拟手段。

Method: 在超导量子处理器上实现(2+1)维Z₂格点规范场论，包含动力学物质场与显式磁面片相互作用；使用63个超导量子比特（12个辅助比特），演化端点电荷为静态或动力学的初始局域弦；空间分辨测量电荷与通量分布及弦路径占据；保留物质与规范自由度以直接获取局部高斯定律约束，并联合辅助比特校验与噪声重标定进行误差检测。

Result: 解禁闭区观察到电荷与通量的快速扩散；在对产生共振附近，弦内部物质占据增强；强电场下扩散被抑制；弦路径占据揭示由面片操作连接的构型间横向重排。在含最多15个二阶Trotter步的线路中完成动力学测量。

Conclusion: 量子模拟是探索二维非微扰弦动力学的实用途径，且规范冗余可作为实验资源用于误差检测。

Abstract: Electric flux strings underpin confinement in gauge theories. In two spatial dimensions, their evolution involves competing processes of charge motion, pair creation, and transverse deformation. Accessing these dynamics beyond perturbative limits challenges classical approaches, as entanglement growth restricts the times accessible to tensor-network simulations. Here we use quantum simulation to investigate a (2+1)D Z2 lattice gauge theory with dynamical matter and an explicit magnetic plaquette interaction, spanning strongly confined and nonperturbative deconfined regimes. Using 63 superconducting qubits, including 12 ancillas, we evolve initially localized strings with static or dynamical endpoint charges. Spatially resolved measurements reveal rapid charge and flux spreading in the deconfined regime, enhanced matter occupation in the string interior near the pair-creation resonance, and suppressed spreading at strong electric fields. String-path occupations further probe transverse rearrangements between configurations connected by plaquette operations. Retaining separate matter and gauge degrees of freedom makes local Gauss-law constraints directly accessible for error detection. Combining these constraints with ancilla checks and noise rescaling, we measure dynamics in circuits containing up to 15 second-order Trotter steps. Our results establish quantum simulation as a practical approach to exploring nonperturbative string dynamics in two spatial dimensions, with gauge redundancy serving as an experimental resource.

</details>


### [94] [Alchemically Transferable Parameters for Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11445)
*Jonas Motyl,Jakob S. Kottmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种"炼金术式"几何投影方法，无需针对有机分子重新训练，即可将已训练的氢体系机器学习线路参数预测模型迁移到有机分子上，每个键型仅需拟合一个投影半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子特征求解器（VQE）的主要瓶颈是经典优化环：贫瘠高原、局部极小、能量与梯度评估代价高昂。用机器学习直接预测线路参数可绕过该瓶颈，但此前模型仅在氢体系内可跨体系尺寸与结构迁移；扩展到有机分子通常被认为需要特定用例的训练集重新训练。作者要证明这种重新训练并不必要。

Method: 建立"炼金术式"（alchemical）迁移方法：通过几何投影，沿目标分子的键方向放置"代理氢原子"，将其局域成键环境映射到小型氢体系碎片上，从而直接复用已有的氢体系求解器（模型参数不变）。唯一新增量为每种键型对应的单个投影半径，该半径在一个小型参考分子上一次性拟合后固定复用。方法在既有可分离对（separable-pair）线路设计上验证，并推广到覆盖量子子空间有效基底的多线路（multi-circuit）方案。

Result: 研究表明，无需有机分子专属训练集即可复用氢体系模型；在可分离对线路设计上成功实现迁移，并可将方法扩展到多线路、跨有效基底的框架中。

Conclusion: 氢体系的机器学习线路参数模型可通过键方向代理氢原子的几何投影迁移至有机分子，仅需每键型一个投影半径，且该半径一次拟合即可复用，从而避免重新训练；该"炼金术"思路对可分离对及多线路线路设计均适用。

Abstract: Machine-learned circuit parameters could remove one of the main bottlenecks of variational quantum eigensolvers: the classical optimization loop, with its typical barren plateaus, local minima, and costly energy and gradient evaluations, is replaced by a direct prediction. Prior work showed that machine-learning models paired with a structured circuit design can transfer across system size and structure within hydrogenic systems. The next step: Extending them to organic molecules would seem to require re-training on use-case-specific training sets. In this work, we show that this is not necessary by establishing an alchemical approach. A geometric projection places surrogate hydrogen atoms along the bond directions of the target molecule and so maps its local bonding environment onto small hydrogenic fragments. Existing hydrogenic solvers can then be reused as they are. The only new quantity is a single projection radius per bond type, which is fitted once on a small reference molecule and reused unchanged thereafter. We demonstrate the approach on the separable-pair circuit designs that have been used in prior work and extend the approach to multi-circuit approaches that span effective bases for quantum subspaces.

</details>

### [95] [A NURBS-enhanced high-order finite-volume method for compressible laminar boundary layers on curved domains with hybrid meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13463)
*Yisu Li,Xucheng Meng,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种NURBS增强的k-exact有限体积法，用于混合网格上定常可压缩Navier–Stokes方程，结合新的壁面边界处理、NURBS高阶曲面表示与dGRP黏性通量，实现曲壁边界层的高保真高阶模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩层流边界层在曲壁面上的黏性剪应力与导热热流集中于薄近壁区，高保真模拟既需物理上合理的壁面边界处理，又需与高阶重构相容的几何表示；传统高阶方法在曲壁附近易失精度或需复杂壁面处理。

Method: 在混合网格上构建NURBS增强的k-exact有限体积格式；采用不要求重构变量与施加边界条件所用变量一致的顺序重构壁面边界处理，将物理壁面条件嵌入高阶离散；用NURBS边界表示高阶刻画曲边界；采用扩散广义黎曼问题（dGRP）黏性通量提升数值鲁棒性。

Result: 多组数值算例验证了所提方法的高阶精度与有效性。

Conclusion: NURBS增强k-exact有限体积法配合解耦式壁面重构处理与dGRP黏性通量，可有效解决曲壁可压缩边界层高阶模拟中的几何精度与壁面处理难题。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of compressible laminar boundary layers on curved domains requires physical wall boundary treatment and geometric representation compatible with high-order reconstruction, since viscous shear stresses and conductive heat fluxes are concentrated in a thin near-wall region. Conventional high-order methods often suffer from loss of accuracy near curved walls or require complicated wall treatments. To address these issues, in this paper, we develop a NURBS-enhanced $k$-exact finite-volume method for the steady compressible Navier--Stokes equations on hybrid meshes. To incorporate physical wall conditions into the high-order discretization, we develop a wall boundary treatment that employs sequential reconstruction without requiring the reconstruction variables to coincide with those used to prescribe the boundary conditions. The NURBS-based boundary representation is used to obtain a high-order accurate description of curved boundaries, and the diffusive generalized Riemann problem (dGRP) viscous flux is employed to enhance numerical robustness. A variety of numerical examples are provided to demonstrate the high-order accuracy and effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [96] [Fully GPU-accelerated semistochastic Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13522)
*Lizhong Fu,Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出全GPU加速的分布式半随机EN-PT2实现：在单次内核遍历中融合激发生成、筛选与PT2加权，并用固定大小的随机相位CountSketch数组替代大规模外行列式交换，从而在保持统计精度的前提下大幅降低内存与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶Epstein-Nesbet微扰理论（EN-PT2）是改进selected CI（SCI）能量的重要工具，但巨大的外行列式空间在GPU集群上带来严重的内存与通信成本，限制了其在大参考波函数上的应用。

Method: 分布式半随机EN-PT2的全GPU实现：将激发生成、行列式筛选和PT2权重累加融合为单次kernel遍历；采用固定尺寸的随机相位CountSketch数组限制内存占用，并以有界集合求和（bounded collective summation）替代大规模外行列式的交换通信。

Result: 在相同目标统计标准误下，单张A100完成EN-PT2计算比64核CPU上的Arrow快8.21–37.53倍；固定负载强扩展测试在跨两节点的16块GPU上达到93.5%并行效率。并成功应用于(60e,187o)活性空间的铁-氧配合物、每态含2亿行列式的铁卟啉参考态以及神经网络量子态。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助GPU加速与sketch压缩通信，EN-PT2可高效地为大规模参考波函数（包括SCI、铁配合物及神经网络量子态）提供微扰修正，为GPU时代的噪声/微扰后处理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Second-order Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory (EN-PT2) is an important tool for improving selected configuration interaction (SCI) energies, but the large external determinant space creates substantial memory and communication costs on GPU clusters. We develop a fully GPU-accelerated implementation of distributed semistochastic EN-PT2, fusing excitation generation, screening, and PT2 weighting in a single kernel traversal. Fixed-size random-phase CountSketch arrays limit memory use and replace large external determinant exchange with bounded collective summation. The implementation on one A100 completes EN-PT2 calculations 8.21-37.53 times faster than Arrow on 64 CPU cores at the same requested statistical standard error, and fixed-workload strong-scaling tests achieve 93.5% parallel efficiency on 16 GPUs across two nodes. Applications to an iron-oxo complex in a (60e, 187o) active space, iron-porphyrin references containing 200 million determinants per state, and neural-network quantum states establish the method's efficiency for perturbative corrections to large reference wave functions in the GPU era.

</details>


### [97] [Neural network scaling and generalization for 3D strain fields in heterogeneous materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14158)
*Lucas Fourel,Paul Devianne,Marine Debouvry,Tristan Destruhaut,Russell Gunn,Ashwini Gupta,Lori Graham-Brady,Jean-François Molinari*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种U-Net代理模型，可在最高256³体素分辨率下预测混凝土细观结构的完整36分量应变局部化张量场，MAE约1.118%，推理较FFT求解器加速约8.7倍，并提出基于MMD的分布外检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算均匀化与耦合多尺度模拟需在代表体积元上反复求解力学场，高分辨率三维微结构下成本过高；现有机器学习代理多针对固定宏观载荷预测场，且缺乏关于如何随分辨率扩展网络架构的指导。

Method: 以FFT基线弹性求解器生成局部化张量训练数据，训练预测36分量张量场的U-Net；进行大规模超参数优化以导出缩放规律与精度-速度权衡，并在Nvidia H100上评测；另在内部特征图上计算MMD指标用于分布外微结构检测。

Result: 仅网络宽度与深度需随问题复杂度扩展，而通道倍增等常规U-Net设计在此问题上次优；最终网络在验证集与独立测试集上MAE均为1.118%，相对FFT求解器加速约8.7倍；泛化实验显示可跨骨料体积分数迁移，训练形貌多样性至关重要。

Conclusion: 针对高分辨率3D张量场预测，需要针对问题定制U-Net缩放规则；MMD特征空间指标可在无需昂贵参考计算的情况下可靠识别分布外微结构，从而提升代理模型的可信度。

Abstract: Computational homogenization and coupled multiscale simulations of heterogeneous materials require repeated solution of mechanical fields on Representative Volume Elements, which becomes prohibitively expensive for high-resolution three-dimensional microstructures. Machine-learning surrogates can accelerate these computations, yet most existing approaches predict fields for fixed macroscopic loadings and provide limited guidance on how to scale network architectures for high-resolution 3D tensor-field prediction. This work presents a U-Net surrogate trained to predict the full 36-component strain localization tensor field in voxelized concrete mesostructures at resolutions up to $256^3$. Training localization tensors are generated with an FFT-based linear elastic solver, and an extensive hyperparameter optimization is performed to derive scaling rules and an accuracy-speed trade-off analysis. The results show that only network width and depth need to be scaled with problem complexity, while several conventional U-Net design choices, such as doubling channels after downsampling, are suboptimal for this problem. The final neural network achieves a Mean Absolute Error of 1.118% on both the validation and the held-out test sets, and an inference speedup of approximately 8.7x over the FFT-based solver on an Nvidia H100 GPU. Generalization experiments reveal transfer across aggregate volume fractions and highlight the importance of training on diverse morphologies. To safeguard reliability, a Maximum Mean Discrepancy metric computed on internal feature maps is proposed for detecting out-of-distribution microstructures without costly reference computations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [An ensemble of graph generative models for assisted history matching of the Brugge field case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12927)
*Gleb Shishaev,Anton Shirokov,Shadfar Davoodi,Mohammed Al-Shargabi,Vasily Demyanov,Daniel Arnold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出基于图自编码器的分区生成模型，为Brugge基准油田的每个沉积/地层带建立联合潜空间，在160维潜空间中用CMA-ES做辅助历史拟合，实现了保地质真实性的历史拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助历史拟合具有非唯一性：多个地质实现可给出等效产量响应，但单井拟合差异显著；当油田包含多个沉积环境与建模策略时，这种歧义进一步加剧。

Method: 图自编码器生成器直接作用于角点网格活跃单元（无需转换为规则格架），每个地层带配专属生成模型并构建联合潜空间；用CMA-ES在160维联合潜空间搜索；目标函数为多分量：静态井数据失配、动态生产失配、基于解码器诱导潜度量的地质真实性惩罚；历史拟合前用拓扑数据分析做潜空间诊断，判断单生成器是否足够、是否需多生成器形式。

Result: 分区形式得到连贯潜空间，保留各带间不同的孔隙度—渗透率关系，历史拟合解保持在所学地质流形内；拟合模型在先验不确定性范围内再现油田产油与产水，井级拟合效果与既往基于EnKF、PCA-PSO、MKL-PSO的Brugge研究相当。

Conclusion: 分区图自编码器联合潜空间框架可有效缓解多沉积环境下历史拟合的非唯一性，在保持地质合理性前提下达到与主流方法可比的拟合质量。

Abstract: Assisted history matching remains non-unique because many geological realisations can reproduce equivalent production responses with substantial differences between individual well matches. This ambiguity increases when a field comprises several depositional settings and modelling strategies. The paper applied a graph autoencoder approach to history matching of a benchmark Brugge field that comprises different depositional zones. Each stratigraphic zone is modelled with its dedicated generative model that comprises a joint latent space for optimisation history matching loops.
