This paper presents neural network-assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), which replaces the multidimensional search at each extraction step with a multi-head classifier while retaining least-squares (LS) estimation of the complex path gain, parametric reconstruction, and residual cancellation. The same classifier is applied to successive residuals, allowing the extraction depth to vary without changing its output structure. With the number of iterations prescribed by the reference multipath-component (MPC) count, off-grid synthetic tests show that NN-CLEAN slightly exceeds grid-search CLEAN (GS-CLEAN) in path accuracy for one and two MPCs, although its accuracy declines more rapidly at larger path counts. For complete three-MPC extraction at a batch size of eight, NN-CLEAN achieves an approximately $27\times$ speedup and a $52$-fold reduction in peak GPU memory relative to GS-CLEAN. Applied without retraining to two-MPC line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) Sionna ray-tracing channels within a coded MIMO link, NN-CLEAN substantially reduces channel-reconstruction mean-squared error (MSE) and bit error rate (BER) relative to interpolated LS channel estimation. GS-CLEAN retains an accuracy advantage on these channels, indicating the estimation cost associated with the computational savings.

</details>


### [132] [Train High, Ship Low: Efficient LoRA Synchronization for Distributed On-Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13306)
*Shenghui Li,Li Ju,Thiemo Voigt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiLo框架：用压缩梯度传播+本地优化器重放替代LoRA参数传输，并设计Spar梯度压缩器，在带宽受限下仅传1.6%字节即保持接近全同步的训练质量，端到端提速最高2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带宽受限的分布式on-policy后训练中，向rollout worker传输LoRA适配器成为瓶颈；降低训练rank会限制工作负载所需容量，而自适应优化器更新难以压缩。

Method: 1) HiLo：压缩器无关框架，worker维护复制优化器状态，由解码后的梯度在本地重放优化器更新，替代参数传输；在可复现执行下learner与worker在同一版本保持一致策略状态。2) Spar：预算感知梯度压缩器，结合谱截断、自适应rank分配与误差反馈，解耦训练rank与通信开销。

Result: 在on-policy蒸馏与奖励引导后训练实验中，HiLo+Spar训练质量接近全同步，传输字节低至适配器的1.6%，带宽受限下端到端更新加速最高2.2×。

Conclusion: 以梯度分发+本地优化器重放替代参数同步是降低LoRA分布式后训练通信瓶颈的有效途径，且可与自适应优化器兼容，实现训练秩与通信成本的解耦。

Abstract: Transmitting LoRA adapters to rollout workers can bottleneck distributed on-policy post-training over bandwidth-constrained networks. Lowering the training rank may limit the capacity a workload needs, while adaptive optimizer updates are difficult to compress. Rollout workers can instead reconstruct updates from gradients by maintaining replicated optimizer states. We introduce HiLo, a compressor-agnostic framework that replaces parameter transmission with compressed gradient dissemination and local optimizer replay. By applying the same decoded gradients, the learner and workers maintain consistent policy states at the same version under reproducible execution. We further develop Spar, a budget-aware gradient compressor that combines spectral truncation, adaptive rank allocation and error feedback to decouple training rank from communication cost. Experiments across on-policy distillation and reward-guided post-training show that HiLo with Spar maintains learning quality close to full synchronization while transmitting as little as 1.6% of the adapter bytes, and accelerates end-to-end updates by up to $2.2\times$ under bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [133] [One Trajectory, Two Requirements: Accuracy and Local Stability in Euler Sampling for Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13311)
*Mahindra Rautela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文质疑流程匹配（flow matching）机器人策略中"离线精度扫描选定的固定Euler步数"这一做法，指出轨迹精度与局部Euler稳定性可能给出不同的步数需求，并用精确二维系统、受控增强实验和冻结π0.5-LIBERO检查点加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略通常沿用离线精度扫描选出的固定Euler步数，但推理时每个输入（视觉观测、机器人状态、语言指令）连同源噪声采样都定义不同的ODE轨迹和局部Jacobian序列。作者因此追问：一个能准确跟随名义噪声到动作轨迹的步数，是否仍可能在强收缩方向上过粗。

Method: 构建精确可解的二维坐标系，并对六个已训练流模型做受控增强，在保持原学习传输不变的前提下分离轨迹精度与局部Euler稳定性两种步数需求；随后在不改动权重和动作动力学的条件下分析冻结的π0.5-LIBERO检查点，取10个LIBERO-10任务共30个策略输入计算Jacobian收缩方向，并按5%与1%终端误差做精度扫描。

Result: 二维系统与六个流模型的受控实验表明，即使原始学习传输未变，轨迹精度与局部Euler稳定性也可给出不同的步数要求。在π0.5-LIBERO上，动作终点附近Jacobian存在强收缩方向，但所有采样模态均未违反Euler非扩张条件；其中5个输入在5%终端误差下需4–9步、1%下需87–112步。

Conclusion: 该局部判据下这五个配对案例与"精度受限而非稳定性受限"的采样一致；但受控实验说明仅凭终点精度无法排除独立的稳定性需求，故固定步数的选取应同时审视局部Euler稳定性。

Abstract: Flow matching robot policies commonly use a fixed Euler step count selected from offline accuracy sweeps. At inference time, however, each policy input (visual observation, robot state, and language instruction) together with a source-noise draw defines a different ODE trajectory and sequence of local Jacobians. We ask whether a step count that accurately follows the nominal noise-to-action trajectory can still be too coarse for strongly contracting directions around it. An exact two-coordinate system and a controlled augmentation of six trained flow models show that trajectory accuracy and local Euler stability can impose different step requirements even when the original learned transport is unchanged. We then study a frozen $π_{0.5}$-LIBERO checkpoint without modifying its weights or action dynamics. Across 30 policy inputs from 10 LIBERO-10 tasks, the Jacobian exhibits strong contracting directions near the action endpoint, yet no sampled mode violates the Euler non-expansion condition. On five of those inputs, accuracy sweeps require 4--9 Euler steps at 5% terminal error and 87--112 at 1%. These five paired cases are therefore consistent with accuracy-limited rather than stability-limited sampling under this local criterion, while the controlled experiments show why endpoint accuracy alone cannot rule out a separate stability requirement.

</details>


### [134] [MicroLens: Read, Trace, and Rewire the Residual Stream with Two Lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13322)
*Nika Zahedi,Fangyi Zhu,Anna Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个交互式可视化工具，将"读取（logit lens/J-lens 解码）"、"溯源（trace）"与"编辑（edit 并重跑模型）"统一到残差流的"层×token"网格上，并在 Gemma-3-4B-it 上展示三种使用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 lens 方法（把隐藏状态解码成词并画成热力图）只能说明某个向量"读出来像什么"，无法区分模型是否、以及多大程度上真正使用了该信息；缺少将读取、溯源与因果编辑结合起来的工具。

Method: 构建交互式查看器：残差流的每一格可用两种 lens（logit lens 与 Jacobian lens）解码、回溯其来源 token，并可编辑该格后即时重跑模型。基于数百条 prompt 的批量扫描，在 Gemma-3-4B-it 上设计三个用例：两种 lens 方向的可写入性、两跳常识题的中间实体定位与替换干预、思维链提示下答案的提前显现。

Result: (1) 两种 lens 给出不同的可写方向：logit lens 的词方向决定 token 的输出，J-lens 方向改变后续 token 的读取；(2) 两跳题中，州/国家信息在城市 token 处最早于第 7 层可读，替换后 92–98% 能改变答案，但在答案位置上该信息未被使用；(3) 逐步推理提示下，正确答案常在推理写出前即已是残差流中首位词：检索类问题 89–92%，多跳与串行类 39–63%，远高于对应干扰项（≤5%），排序类谜题是例外，须写出最终推理步骤才解出。此外，对可见答案，J-lens 比 logit lens 早 1–2 层读出。

Conclusion: 将读取、溯源与编辑集成在同一网格上，能把"表示了什么"与"模型实际使用了什么"区分开来，从而揭示 lens 方向的功能差异、中间实体的因果作用以及答案在残差流中的提前出现规律；排序类任务表明推理并非总是预先完成。

Abstract: Lenses that decode hidden states into words are a popular way to look inside language models, but a heatmap of decoded words only shows what a vector reads as and cannot distinguish whether, or how much, the model uses the information. We present an interactive viewer that puts reading, tracing and editing on the same layers-by-tokens grid of the residual stream. In the viewer, every cell can be decoded with two lenses, the logit lens and the Jacobian lens (J-lens), traced back to the tokens it drew from, and edited, with the rest of the model rerun on the spot. We demonstrate three usage scenarios on Gemma-3-4B-it, backed by sweeps over a few hundred prompts, to show what this tool can discover. First, the two lenses give different directions to write with, each suiting a different edit: the logit lens's word direction sets what a token outputs, while the J-lens's direction changes what later tokens read. Second, in two-hop trivia questions (the capital of the state or country containing a given city), the state or country is readable at the city token from as early as layer 7, and swapping it there can redirect the answer 92-98% of the time; however, at the answer position, the state or country information is not used. Third, when a chat model answers questions with a step-by-step reasoning prompt, the correct answer is often already the top word in the residual stream before any reasoning is written: for 89-92% of retrieval questions and 39-63% of multi-hop and serial ones, far above their respective distractors (at most 5%). However, ordering puzzles are an exception, as the model works them out only once it writes its final reasoning steps. Furthermore, for answers that are visible in the residual stream, the J-lens reads them a layer or two earlier than the logit lens across the different types of question.

</details>


### [135] [Robust Flow Matching under Target Corruption and Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13324)
*Mert Can Kurucu,Erik Englesson,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将流匹配（FM）训练视为条件向量场的时间相关回归问题，分析目标数据损坏与标签噪声两类污染对目标函数的影响，并提出基于污染特异性残差得分的样本加权方法（TRS 与 CCS）以提升 FM 的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FM 依赖"干净目标数据与干净标签"的假设，而实际场景中数据损坏与标签噪声普遍存在；现有鲁棒训练方法未针对 FM 的连续时间向量场回归结构进行设计，缺乏对污染如何影响 FM 目标的理论刻画。

Method: 将 FM 训练重构为条件向量场上的时间相关回归问题，分析两种污染的影响：目标损坏引入随时间增长的时间相关残差项；标签噪声导致模型更新到错误的类条件向量场。据此把污染特异性残差得分映射为样本权重：针对目标损坏提出 Temporal Residual Score（TRS），强调中间至后期 FM 时间步的残差幅值（该区间干净样本与损坏样本更易区分）；针对标签噪声提出 Comparative Conditional Score（CCS），在同一轨迹与速度目标下，将观测到的类条件向量场残差与其他类条件向量场的残差进行比较。

Result: 在可控污染设置与真实噪声标签上的实验表明，所提方法相较标准 FM 及鲁棒训练基线均提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 污染特异性的残差结构可作为构建鲁棒 FM 的实用基础。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) is a strong framework for generative modeling due to its stable training and efficient sampling. However, FM assumes clean target data and labels, an assumption often violated in practice. We formulate FM training as a time-dependent regression problem over conditional vector fields and analyze how the two corruption types affect the objective: i) target corruption adds a time-dependent residual term that grows with time, ii) label noise causes updates to the wrong class-conditional vector field. Based on this analysis, we propose to improve FM robustness by mapping corruption-specific residual scores to sample weights. For target corruption, we propose the Temporal Residual Score (TRS), which emphasizes residual magnitudes at intermediate-to-late FM times, where clean and corrupted samples are more separable. For label noise, we propose the Comparative Conditional Score (CCS), which compares the residual of the observed class-conditional vector field against alternative class-conditional vector fields for the same trajectory and velocity target. Experiments across controlled corruption settings and real-world noisy labels show that the proposed methods improve robustness over standard FM and robust-training baselines, supporting corruption-specific residual structure as a practical basis for robust FM.

</details>


### [136] [Stochastic Projections and Rank-Aware Learning Rates for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13331)
*Saber Malekmohammadi,Kevin Kuo,Virginia Smith,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把 LoRA 的 A 矩阵冻结并周期性重采样，证明该做法等价于对全量微调做带秩相关扰动的随机近似，据此提出按秩缩放学习率的联邦微调方法 SPRA-LoRA，在客户端开销减半的前提下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的分布式客户端上微调大规模基础模型面临计算与通信双重瓶颈。LoRA + 联邦学习虽提供了秩级灵活性，但 A、B 两个适配矩阵相互耦合，使优化复杂化，且上行传输与本地计算开销仍高。

Method: 理论分析：证明高斯初始化、A 冻结的 LoRA 微调相当于全量微调的随机近似，批梯度上叠加了随秩变化的随机扰动；并证明在此"冻结 A"机制下，信噪比（SNR）随适配秩 r 线性增长。方法：提出 SPRA-LoRA，客户端冻结 A 并周期性重采样，解耦 A/B 梯度，节省 50% 计算与上行通信开销以换取更高秩；同时令学习率随秩动态缩放，并在异构客户端资源下给出收敛性证明。

Result: 冻结 A 使计算与上行通信开销各减半；SNR 与秩 r 呈线性关系，说明学习率需按秩缩放；SPRA-LoRA 在基准数据集上以客户端一半的开销持续优于 SOTA 基线，且在异构资源下收敛性得到理论保证。

Conclusion: 冻结并重采样 A 是解耦 LoRA 优化、降低联邦微调开销的有效手段；秩 r 通过 SNR 直接影响梯度质量，故秩感知的学习率缩放必要且有效，SPRA-LoRA 在效率与性能上同时取得改进。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large-scale foundation models across resource-constrained, distributed clients presents significant computational and communication challenges. While merging Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with Federated Learning (FL) enables rank-based flexibility, the interdependence of the adaptation matrices ($\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$) often complicates optimization. In this work, we theoretically demonstrate that LoRA fine-tuning with Gaussian initialization and a frozen $\mathbf{A}$ matrix acts as a stochastic approximation of full fine-tuning, where batch gradients are subject to a random, rank-dependent perturbation. Motivated by this insight, we propose freezing and periodically resampling $\mathbf{A}$ matrices at the client level. This strategy decouples the $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ gradients, diminishes LoRA low-rank constraints and reduces both computational and uplink communication overheads by 50\%, allowing for the reallocation of resources toward higher adaptation ranks. Furthermore, we show that in this frozen-$\mathbf{A}$ regime, the adaptation rank $r$ directly governs the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of the perturbed gradients. Specifically, we find that the SNR increases linearly with $r$, implying that clients' learning rates must be dynamically scaled with their adaptation ranks for higher utility. Building on these foundations, we introduce SPRA-LoRA (Stochastic Projection Rank-Aware LoRA), a federated rank-aware fine-tuning strategy that utilizes frozen, resampled $\mathbf{A}$ matrices and rank-dependent learning rates for clients. We also prove its convergence with heterogeneous client resources. Our experiments across benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPRA-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with half the overhead on clients.

</details>


### [137] [Advancing Mass-Conserving Deep Learning for Distributed Hydrologic Modeling: A Case Study in a Snow-Dominated Watershed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13346)
*Yihan Wang,Lujun Zhang,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yang Hong,Tiantian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PECAN——一种基于卷积LSTM的分布式质量守恒水文模型框架，可诊断物理约束深度学习的结构性缺陷；在科罗拉多雪域流域验证其径流模拟能力与土壤湿度表征的合理性，同时揭示其对积雪过程的错误刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有质量守恒神经网络多为集总式（lumped）表述，对"物理约束何时、为何导致不合理水文行为"只能提供零散且间接的证据，缺乏分布式框架下对内部状态的显式诊断能力。

Method: 构建PECAN（Physics-Enhanced Conservation-Augmented Net），将质量守恒嵌入卷积LSTM实现分布式水文建模，含两种变体：PECAN-MC（基于降水的严格守恒约束）与PECAN-MCR（放宽约束、允许降水外额外水分输入）。仅用站点径流训练，在科罗拉多雪域流域测试，并与集总DL基线和分布式过程模型VIC进行对比，同时检验细胞状态与参考土壤湿度的一致性。

Result: PECAN在出口径流模拟精度上具竞争力，并能输出空间显式的潜在状态；其细胞状态与参考土壤湿度在时间动态上相似，具有物理可解释性。但分析发现PECAN对积雪过程表征错误——过早将冬季雪水当量移出系统，而非滞留至春季释放。

Conclusion: 在分布式框架下，质量守恒神经网络可兼顾径流模拟精度与内部状态诊断能力，并能暴露物理约束模型在过程表征上的结构性局限，为未来水文物理感知神经网络的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Recent progress in physics-aware deep learning (DL) has embedded mass conservation within Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) architecture for hydrologic modeling, improving physical consistency and enabling diagnostics of internal states. However, existing mass-conserving neural networks are primarily developed in lumped formulations. Consequently, previous studies provide only fragmented and often indirect evidence of when and why imposed physical constraints may lead to unrealistic hydrologic behavior. To address this limitation, we propose Physics-Enhanced Conservation-Augmented Net (PECAN), a mass-conserving convolutional LSTM-based framework for distributed hydrologic modeling and diagnosing structural limitations of physics-aware DL models. PECAN is implemented in two variants: (1) PECAN-MC with a strict precipitation-based conservation constraint and (2) PECAN-MCR with a relaxed conservation constraint allowing additional water inputs beyond precipitation. Both variants are trained solely on gauge streamflow and evaluated in a snow-dominated watershed in Colorado. We benchmark PECAN against lumped DL baselines and the distributed process-based Variable Infiltration Capacity (VIC) model. Results show that PECAN achieves competitive outlet streamflow simulation accuracy while producing spatially explicit latent states for interpretation. Comparisons with reference soil moisture indicate that PECAN cell states resemble meaningful temporal soil moisture dynamics. However, analyses also reveal that PECAN misrepresents snow processes by prematurely removing winter snow water storage from the system rather than retaining it for spring release. PECAN demonstrates accurate streamflow simulation at gauges and shows diagnostic potential to expose structural limitations in hydrologic process representation, thereby informing future physics-aware neural network development for hydrology.

</details>


### [138] [When Are Predictions Enough? An Evaluation Protocol for Frozen Expert Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13356)
*Alessandro Pereira,Keith J Ransom,Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套可复用的评估协议，在21个冻结深度伪造检测器上比较"特征组合"与"预测组合"两种专家融合方式，指出评估设计（基线强度、重叠控制、划分方式）会显著改变结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常将独立训练、权重冻结的模型通过最终预测进行组合，但专家内部特征可能保留预测所遗漏的信息。作者追问：何时特征组合优于预测组合，以及如何使这一比较公平。

Method: 构建可复用评估协议并应用于DF40的21个冻结检测器，并审计评估选择对结论的影响。协议包含：强预测基线、简单特征组合器与大网络的对比、分别变化架构与训练数据、按留出生成器报告结果并控制训练/测试重叠。

Result: 在留一生成器评估下，对6个架构多样专家特征的逻辑回归，比每折中最佳预测组合器宏AUROC高0.081，8个生成器中有7个获益；约3.5M参数的网络相比10K参数线性模型无明显提升。在受重叠影响的标准划分上削弱预测基线，可虚假放大特征优势至0.172 AUROC；重叠感知评估也改变增益估计。

Conclusion: 当特征保留预测所遗漏且可迁移到新生成器的信息时，特征组合有益；若最强预测基线本身已表现良好，预测可能足够；两者均无法可靠弥补专家迁移能力不足。缺乏上述控制时，同一基准可支持不同结论。

Abstract: Practitioners often combine independently trained models while keeping their weights frozen. These experts are commonly combined through their final predictions, although their internal features may retain useful information that those predictions omit. We ask when combining features improves on combining predictions, and how to make that comparison fair. We introduce a reusable evaluation protocol, apply it to 21 frozen deepfake detectors from DF40 (Yan et al., 2024), and audit how evaluation choices change the conclusions. The protocol uses strong prediction baselines, compares simple and larger feature combiners, varies architecture and training data separately, and reports results by held-out generator with train/test overlap controls. Under leave-one-generator-out evaluation, logistic regression on the features of six architecture-diverse experts improves macro AUROC by 0.081 over the best tested prediction combiner in each fold, with gains on seven of eight generators. The larger network shows no clear improvement over the linear model (approximately 3.5M versus 10K parameters), suggesting that access to richer information matters more than additional combiner capacity on this pool. Features may help when they retain information that the predictions omit and that transfers to new generators. Predictions may be enough when the strongest prediction baseline already performs well, while neither approach reliably compensates for poor expert transfer. Weakening prediction baselines on the overlap-affected standard split inflates the apparent feature advantage by up to 0.172 AUROC. Overlap-aware evaluation also changes the estimated gain, though the contrast does not isolate overlap. Results by generator and expert pool show where the advantage holds, weakens, or reverses. Without these controls, the same benchmark can support different conclusions.

</details>


### [139] [Reconstructing Boundary Layer Wind Profiles with Machine Learning: A Comparative Study on IGRA Radiosonde Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13359)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov,Egor Bugaev,Vladimir Gordin,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模IGRA探空数据上系统比较表格学习模型与传统解析参数化方法对大气边界层风廓线的预测能力，发现精度主要由预测因子而非模型架构决定，且存在约1.77 m/s的经验误差下限，并用一维FNO做后处理显著改善近地面误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气边界层风廓线对短时预报与风能选址至关重要，但无线电探空覆盖稀疏，实际中只能用少量物理预测因子插值，因此需要明确现代学习方法相对解析参数化的收益来源与性能上限。

Method: 在158,097条IGRA探空样本上，将CatBoost、TabM、FT-Transformer等现代表格学习器与幂律、Monin–Obukhov解析参数化对比；在固定预测因子集下进行架构消融；通过分箱内廓线离散度的队列分析估计经验误差下限；并将一维傅里叶神经算子（FNO）作为冻结表格预测的后验校正器。

Result: CatBoost、TabM与FT-Transformer将RMSE降至1.3–1.6 m/s，而幂律与Monin–Obukhov拟合约为3 m/s；固定预测因子集后三种架构差异小于0.05 m/s。使用标准探空特征的点式预测器存在约1.77 m/s的经验下限。FNO后验校正在美国站点将近地面RMSE从1.5 m/s降至1.2 m/s，上层误差不变。

Conclusion: 模型架构已非瓶颈，精度受限于输入特征信息量；进一步提升应依赖新预测因子的引入，而非更大规模的模型，FNO类算子可用于局部近地面精化。

Abstract: Wind profiles in the atmospheric boundary layer (ABL) drive short-range forecasting and wind-energy siting, yet sparse radiosonde coverage forces practitioners to interpolate them from a handful of physical predictors. We benchmark modern tabular learners against analytical parameterizations on 158,097 IGRA soundings. CatBoost, TabM, and FT-Transformer reduce RMSE to 1.3-1.6 m/s, against approximately 3 m/s for power-law and Monin-Obukhov fits; once the predictor set is fixed the three architectures differ by less than 0.05 m/s, so accuracy is driven by predictors rather than architecture. A cohort analysis of within-bin profile dispersion places an empirical floor of approximately 1.77 m/s on any point-wise predictor that uses standard radiosonde features, consistent with prior eddy-viscosity inversions. A one-dimensional Fourier Neural Operator applied as a post-hoc corrector to frozen tabular predictions reduces near-surface RMSE on U.S. stations from 1.5 to 1.2 m/s, while leaving upper-layer error unchanged. Further gains should come from new predictors rather than larger models.

</details>


### [140] [Looping Deeper Without Growing the KV Cache: Recurrent Depth for Causal Encoder-Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13378)
*Jiaqi Leng,Minshen Zhang,Zhaofeng Sun,Yu-Yang Qian,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环式因果编码器-解码器（Looped CED），通过跨层与跨轮的KV共享实现零轮次KV增长，在极小KV缓存下获得循环深度带来的性能收益并在大规模部署中显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型可在不增加参数的情况下加深计算，但跨层、跨轮缓存完整上下文的KV状态会严重限制服务端批处理规模，需要解决"在非对称堆栈中何处、如何循环"的问题。

Method: 设计仅由稀疏编码器层产生全局KV的非对称结构；将循环置于编码器中，迭代精炼全局KV表示而非重复读取静态解码器投影；在随机循环深度下训练编码器循环，诱导出定点均衡，使每轮无损共享最后一轮的KV缓存，并与跨层KV共享叠加。

Result: Looped CED达到与非循环CED相同的最小内存占用，以1/6的全局KV恢复循环Transformer 86-93%的增益；在552B参数模型上循环8轮：单节点即可容纳38个128K请求（与非循环模型相当），每token KV减少36.8倍，8K提示吞吐提升3.6倍。

Conclusion: 循环应放在编码器而非解码器侧以精炼全局KV；随机深度训练带来可无损共享KV的定点均衡，使循环深度与高效服务兼容，并在生产规模上验证了可行性。

Abstract: Looped language models deepen computation without adding parameters, yet caching full-context key-value (KV) states across layers and rounds severely bounds serving batch size. We design a looped causal encoder-decoder (CED) that achieves recurrent depth with zero round-wise KV growth, addressing where and how to loop an asymmetric stack in which only sparse encoder layers generate global KV. We find that recurrence must be placed in the encoder to iteratively refine global KV representations rather than re-reading static decoder projections, which both drives language-modeling gains and preserves long-context scaling. Furthermore, training the encoder loop under stochastic recurrence depth induces a fixed-point equilibrium where every round losslessly shares the last round's KV cache. Compounding cross-layer and cross-round KV sharing, the resulting Looped CED matches an unlooped CED's minimal memory footprint and recovers 86-93% of a looped Transformer's gain with 1/6 of its global KV. To test serving at production scale, we loop the 552B-parameter DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash for 8 rounds on one 4xGB200 node: with per-layer and per-round KV the loop cannot fit a single 128K-token request, whereas Looped CED holds 36.8x less KV per token, fits 38 such requests (as many as the unlooped model), and serves 3.6x higher throughput on 8K-token prompts.

</details>


### [141] [Settling the Burn-in Gap: Sharp Risk Bounds for Tail-Averaged SGD in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13383)
*Qingyue Zhao,Xuheng Li,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维线性回归中常数步长在线SGD（尾平均）的超额风险，在Zou等(2021)的实例相关谱框架下建立了初始化显式的匹配上下界，并证明该框架原有的上下界均是松的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有分析在有效方差上存在根本性缺口：Zou等(2021)的上界在头部特征空间I_{0:k†}中保留了初始误差的未衰减贡献，而下界则完全忽略了头部初始化诱导的方差，二者不一致，需要澄清初始误差在常数步长SGD中如何真实传播与衰减。

Method: 基于总体协方差谱H与初始化w_0-w*的实例相关谱框架，在轨迹谱反馈条件下推导尾平均SGD总超额风险的初始化显式上下界；证明谱反馈条件在两类常数步长机制下成立：任意谱与有限时间下的事先选定、依赖时间步长；以及多项式衰减谱λ_i≍i^{-a}(a>1)下单一与时间无关的正步长。并以一维、二维分离构造佐证。

Result: 获得与现有界匹配且更紧的初始化显式上下界：头部初始化的单跳快模态方差在预热阶段指数衰减；而预热早期从头部散射到慢谱模态的四阶矩能量则未衰变地进入平均窗口。构造显示Zou等(2021)下界存在指数与多项式之间的差距，且仅有四阶矩假设无法确定渐近指数衰减率。

Conclusion: 常数步长尾平均SGD的超额风险由头部初始化的快模态（指数衰减）与散射入慢模态的四阶矩能量（持久存活）共同刻画；谱反馈条件在广泛步长机制下可实现匹配界；仅凭四阶矩信息不足，需要额外结构条件才能确定渐近指数衰减率。

Abstract: We study the excess risk of constant-stepsize online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with tail-averaging for high-dimensional linear regression. Building on the instance-dependent spectral framework of Zou et al. (2021), we establish matching initialization-explicit upper and lower bounds on the total excess risk of tail-averaged SGD in terms of the population covariance spectrum $\mathbf{H}$ and initialization $\mathbf{w}_0-\mathbf{w}^*$ whenever a trajectory spectral feedback condition holds. Existing analyses exhibit a fundamental gap in the effective variance: the upper bound of Zou et al. (2021) retains an undamped contribution from the initial error in the head eigenspace $\mathbf{I}_{0:k^\dagger}$, whereas their lower bound omits the variance induced by the head initialization altogether. We show that both the existing upper and lower bounds are loose: the single-hop fast-mode variance contribution from the head initialization is exponentially damped during the burn-in phase, whereas fourth-moment energy scattered from the head initialization into slow spectral modes during the early burn-in steps survives undamped into the averaging window. We prove that our spectral feedback condition is satisfied in two concrete constant-stepsize regimes: (i) for arbitrary covariance spectra $\mathbf{H}$ and any finite horizon, under an explicit horizon-dependent stepsize chosen prior to execution; and (ii) for polynomially decaying spectra $λ_i\asymp i^{-a}$ ($a>1$), under a single horizon-independent positive stepsize. Finally, we complement these positive results with explicit one- and two-dimensional separation constructions proving an exponential-versus-polynomial gap in the lower bound of Zou et al. (2021) and showing why fourth-moment assumptions alone cannot determine the asymptotic exponential decay rate without additional structural conditions.

</details>


### [142] [AdjointFlow: Accelerating Scientific Inverse Problems with Physics-Based Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13389)
*Ethan Hanold,Nicholas M. Boffi,Qin Li,Leonardo Zepeda-Núñez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdjointFlow将已知物理模型(伴随初始化)注入随机插值生成输运过程，实现高精度、低成本的逆问题求解，比条件扩散模型更准，比PnP方法快至多五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逆问题求解器受非凸性与昂贵物理模型反复评估困扰；扩散模型虽可离线摊销计算，但纯数据驱动条件模型未充分利用已知物理，且在目标分布集中时输运映射病态；PnP方法利用物理模型引导先验改善数据一致性，却重新引入昂贵迭代计算。

Method: 基于随机插值框架，将物理模型注入生成输运本身：1）设计依赖数据的、基于伴随的初始化，缩短到目标的输运距离；2）修正输运速度，使终点分布集中时仍保持正则性；3）参数化网络，将初始化直接嵌入学习到的速度场。可选推理时细化通过查询物理模型进一步提升数据一致性，无需重训练。

Result: 在逆散射、地震成像与黑洞成像实验中，AdjointFlow的重建精度持续优于条件扩散模型，推理速度比PnP方法快多达五个数量级；可选推理时细化可匹配或超越PnP方法的精度。

Conclusion: 将已知物理结构内嵌于生成输运过程可在精度与效率间取得更优权衡，为逆问题提供了一种兼顾数据一致性与计算成本的生成式求解范式。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous across scientific applications ranging from seismic imaging to astrophysics to nondestructive testing, yet classical solvers often struggle with nonconvexity and repeated evaluations of expensive physical models. Diffusion models can amortize this computation offline, but largely data-driven conditional models underutilize known physics and suffer from ill-conditioned transport maps when the target distribution is concentrated. Plug-and-play (PnP) methods explicitly leverage the physical model to guide a learned prior, improving data consistency but reintroducing costly iterative computations. We instead propose AdjointFlow, a framework built on stochastic interpolants that injects the known physics into the generative transport itself. We design a data-dependent, adjoint-based initialization to reduce transport distance to the target, modify the transport velocity to remain regular as the endpoint distributions concentrate, and parameterize the network to build the initialization directly into the learned velocity. Together, these enable accurate reconstruction at substantially lower cost than generative models starting from Gaussian noise. Experiments on inverse scattering, seismic imaging, and black hole imaging show that AdjointFlow consistently produces more accurate reconstructions than conditional diffusion models while being up to five orders of magnitude faster at inference than PnP methods. Optional inference-time refinement that queries the physical model can further improve data consistency, matching or exceeding the accuracy of PnP methods without retraining.

</details>


### [143] [Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13414)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj,Shahab Rahimirad,Aliasghar Pourghani,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Abolfazl Hashemi,Joseph Campbell,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者指出，SMC功率采样解码在过程中已估计出功率分布的归一化常数，因此可零额外成本地得到序列级R'enyi熵（SRE），并借助R'enyi链式法则扩展为一族任意阶的逐步熵；其中1/2阶熵累积每步搜索代价的对数，在13个设置中的10个为最佳零成本选择预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对整条LLM补全的不确定性量化对选择性预测与自适应计算很关键，但现有零成本信号（平均token熵、log概率）只反映局部或单条补全信息，而自一致性方法需额外多次采样，代价高。作者希望找到一种零边际成本的序列级不确定性度量。

Method: 利用SMC（序贯蒙特卡洛）解码做power sampling时天然估计出的功率分布配分函数，直接读出序列级R'enyi熵（SRE），且在任意粒子预算下都有保证；再用R'enyi链式法则导出任意阶的逐步熵族。理论上证明高于1阶的熵与每步最可能token绑定（故与log概率走势相近），而1/2阶成员累积每步搜索努力的对数。

Result: 1/2阶SRE在13个设置中10个为最佳零成本选择性预测信号。在MATH500上，四个模型及按长度分位内，AUROC比log概率高0.022–0.044，且逐设置留一验证仍选中同一阶数。8粒子探针可识别受益于更多粒子的题目，两遍策略在三个随机种子上以少用34.2%粒子达到与64粒子相当的MATH500准确率。

Conclusion: SMC解码的配分函数可被"免费"转化为序列级R'enyi熵族，为选择性预测提供优于log概率的零成本信号，并可用于自适应粒子分配以节省计算。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R'enyi entropy (SRE) of the model's completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R'enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

</details>


### [144] [You Don't Need Feature Learning to Do In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13431)
*Joseph Turnbull,Jamie Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过核机（旋转不变核）在 ICL 线性回归任务上的谱分析，揭示"记忆 vs. 泛化"的连续谱式学习图景：泛化解只是记忆解的低阶截断，故学习泛化解实为对记忆解的欠拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 ICL 中记忆与泛化的关系及二者何时发生，需要一个可解析的简化模型来刻画样本量增长时的学习动力学。

Method: 在 Raventos et al. (2023) 提出的 ICL 线性回归任务上，研究旋转不变核机；将任务在核特征基下分解，得到学习的谱图像，并分析记忆解与泛化解的阶数结构及其与训练任务集高阶统计量的对应关系。

Result: 随样本量增长，核机依次学到记忆解的更高阶分量，这些分量依赖训练任务集的更高阶统计量；泛化解近似为记忆解的低阶截断，因此模型先泛化、后随训练退化为记忆。此外，实证训练的 Transformer 通过核理论所预测的高阶统计量记忆任务。

Conclusion: 三点结论：(1) ICL 任务上"泛化"解本质是对记忆解的欠拟合；(2) 核机能在此任务上泛化，说明特征学习对 ICL 非必需；(3) Transformer 的经验行为与核理论预测一致，暗示该理论可推广至具备特征学习能力的模型。

Abstract: How should we understand memorization vs. generalization in in-context learning (ICL) tasks, and when does one happen vs. the other? To investigate, we study how rotation-invariant kernel machines learn the ICL linear regression task proposed by Raventos et al. (2023). By decomposing the task in the kernel eigenbasis, we obtain a spectral picture of learning in this ICL setting. This picture reveals that as sample size grows, kernel machines learn successively-higher-degree components of the memorizing solution, which depend upon successively-higher-degree statistics of the training task set. The generalizing solution is approximately a low-degree truncation of the memorizing solution, and so the model first generalizes, then degrades into memorization as training proceeds.
  The takeaways are threefold. First, if this case is indicative, learning a "generalizing" solution on an ICL task is really just underfitting the memorizing solution! Second, the fact that kernel machines can generalize on this task means that feature learning is inessential to ICL. Third, transformers empirically trained to do ICL are shown to memorize tasks through the higher-order statistics of the training tasks that the kernel-based theory predicts, suggesting it applies even to models capable of feature learning.

</details>


### [145] [Fermat ProbCover: Coverage-Based Active Learning on Fermat Distance Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13448)
*Gokul Bhusal,Delanna Do,Kevin Miller,Ekaterina Merkurjev,Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PC-Fermat：用Fermat距离（数据自适应）替代欧氏距离定义覆盖邻域，并通过带权欧氏邻域图+截断最短路算法计算Fermat覆盖图，给出与完全图Fermat覆盖等价的充分条件；在8个分类数据集上多数优于现有主动学习方法，尤其极低预算下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算场景下基于不确定性的主动学习方法常失效，基于覆盖的方法（如ProbCover）更有效；但ProbCover依赖欧氏距离，难以刻画数据的内在几何与分布结构，因此希望引入能反映数据分布的数据自适应距离来改进覆盖式选择。

Method: 1）将ProbCover的欧氏覆盖邻域替换为由Fermat距离（数据自适应、反映数据点分布的距离）定义的邻域，构成PC-Fermat；2）为降低计算开销，在带权欧氏邻域图上用截断最短路算法计算Fermat覆盖图；3）理论分析给出该图构造能精确保持完全图上Fermat覆盖的充分条件。

Result: 在八个分类数据集、不同标注预算（重点为极低预算）下评估：PC-Fermat在大多数数据集上优于多种现有主动学习方法。

Conclusion: 将覆盖式主动学习中的距离度量由欧氏距离推广到数据自适应的Fermat距离是有效且可行的；截断最短路近似在给定条件下保持覆盖的精确性，且方法在低预算主动学习中有实证优势。

Abstract: Active learning aims to reduce annotation cost by selecting the most informative examples for labeling. In the low-budget regime, where uncertainty-based methods often fail, coverage-based active learning methods such as ProbCover have been shown to be effective. However, these methods typically rely on Euclidean distance, which may not adequately capture the underlying geometry of data. In this article, we propose PC-Fermat, a coverage-based active learning method that replaces Euclidean coverage neighborhoods with neighborhoods defined by a Fermat distance, i.e., a data-adaptive distance that reflects the underlying distribution of the data points. To facilitate computation, we construct a weighted Euclidean neighborhood graph and use a truncated shortest-path algorithm to compute the Fermat coverage graph. We also establish sufficient conditions under which this construction exactly preserves the Fermat coverage computed on the complete graph. Finally, we evaluate PC-Fermat on eight classification datasets across varying labeling budgets, with particular emphasis on the very low-budget regime. Our experimental results demonstrate that PC-Fermat outperforms several existing active learning methods on the majority of the datasets.

</details>


### [146] [SLIC: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning Small LMs for Multi-Turn Analog Circuit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13452)
*Yan Xu,Zhengqi Gao,Ching-Yun Ko,Zhang-Wei Hong,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Xin Zhang,Duane S. Boning,Ruonan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLIC：用强化微调训练 4B 本地可部署小模型完成闭环模拟电路优化，仅用开源 65nm PTM 训练，即在四大指标 Pass@1 上达 87.1%，超 763B 模型 48.5 个百分点，并跨未见图拓扑与五个未见工艺节点迁移，甚至迁移至商用 PDK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟 IC 设计需在耦合性能折衷上迭代推理，且依赖仿真器闭环；但商用 PDK 属专有数据，难以安全地调外部托管大模型，导致实际设计场景难以落地。为此需一个可本地部署的小模型方案。

Method: 提出 SLIC 强化微调框架：结构化电路表示 + 离散化相对更新动作；设计域约束与成功率门控的多目标奖励；提出 Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS)，将每步仿真器反馈转化为策略学习信号与在线 rollout 构造信号，以应对稀疏高约束奖励。并发布含 41 个放大器拓扑的目标条件基准，含分布内验证、留出拓扑评估与跨工艺节点迁移三类标准协议。奖励：随机/softmax 两种变体。

Result: 仅用开源 65nm CMOS PTM 训练：SLIC-Random 八轮内分布内四指标 Pass@1 达 87.1%，较 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think (763B) 高 48.5 个百分点；SLIC-Softmax 在留出拓扑上 Pass@1 72.6%；策略无需再微调即迁移到 5 个未见工艺节点；迁移至商用 TSMC 65nm PDK 时，SLIC-Random 留出拓扑 Pass@1 为 49.9%；测试时仿真交互增加可继续提升，32 轮预算下留出拓扑 Pass@1 达 83.2%。

Conclusion: 小规模本地模型经仿真器接地的强化微调，可在模拟电路设计中达到甚至超越超大托管模型，并具备跨拓扑、跨节点的泛化与商用 PDK 本地推理部署能力，避免专有数据外传；性能随测试时计算线性受益。

Abstract: Analog integrated circuit (IC) design is a multi-turn, simulator-grounded engineering task that demands iterative reasoning over coupled performance tradeoffs, yet proprietary process design kits (PDKs) make hosted large language model workflows difficult to deploy in practical design settings. We present SLIC (Small LM Iterating on Circuits), a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that trains a 4B-scale, locally deployable language model for closed-loop analog circuit optimization. SLIC pairs structured circuit representations with discretized relative-update actions, domain- and success-gated multiobjective rewards, and Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS), which turns per-step simulator feedback into signals for both policy learning and online rollout construction in this sparse, highly constrained domain. We further release an open, goal-conditioned benchmark of 41 amplifier topologies with standardized protocols for in-distribution validation, held-out topology evaluation, and cross-technology-node transfer. Trained solely with open source 65 nm CMOS predictive technology models (PTM), SLIC-Random achieves 87.1% in-distribution four-specification Pass@1 within eight turns, surpassing the 763B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think by 48.5 percentage points. Beyond the training distribution, SLIC-Softmax reaches 72.6% Pass@1 on held-out topologies, and the trained policies consistently transfer across five unseen technology nodes without additional fine-tuning. This transfer extends to a commercial TSMC 65 nm CMOS PDK, where SLIC-Random achieves 49.9% held-out-topology Pass@1, demonstrating practical deployment through local inference without transmitting proprietary data to external LLM services. Performance further scales with test-time simulator interactions, reaching up to 83.2% held-out-topology Pass@1 at a 32-turn budget.

</details>


### [147] [Deep Personalization: Continuous Context Observation for Local Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457)
*Yijia Shao,Song Bian,Junxian Guo,Jindong Jiang,Zhen Dong,Huizi Mao,Diyi Yang,Song Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 "deep personalization"（深度个性化）闭环框架：智能体持续观察用户活动、选择性保留长期上下文，并从隐式反馈中学习介入时机，在 Coco 桌面主动代理上实现，端侧运行，OSWorld-Persona 基准上 macro F1 从 0.12–0.19 提升至 0.35–0.48（2.4–3.0 倍），一周用户研究中 F1 由 0.199 升至 0.515。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户难以主动识别何时该求助，导致大量可获得的帮助未被实现；主动式代理虽有望弥补这一缺口，但有效主动性需理解用户实时活动并个性化介入时机，避免打扰。现有方法缺乏持续观察—持续适应的闭环机制，也难以在不泄露原始活动数据的前提下本地部署。

Method: 提出 deep personalization：将连续上下文观察与代理持续自适应连接为闭环。在主动桌面代理 Coco 中由独立的 Observer Agent 监测桌面活动、维护紧凑的长期用户建模上下文，并从参与、忽略、显式请求、重复工作模式等隐式信号中学习"何时介入有益"。同时设计高效端侧策略，使 Observer Agent 可在本地 AI 计算平台运行，无需将原始活动数据传出设备。为在规模上评估且不采集敏感真实用户活动，构建数据合成流水线，将现有 computer-use 轨迹转化为带 persona 条件化介入标签的 OSWorld-Persona 基准。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Persona 上，三个模型骨干的 macro F1 由直接提示的 0.12–0.19 提升至自适应后的 0.35–0.48，相对提升 2.4–3.0 倍；为期一周的用户研究中，Coco 的 macro 平均 F1 从 0.199 提升至 0.515。

Conclusion: 端侧深度个性化可使主动式代理的介入更及时，并随使用逐步贴合每位用户的日常工作习惯；隐式反馈驱动的闭环适应与本地化部署是提升主动性的可行路径。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, users must still recognize when and how to ask for help, leaving many opportunities for useful support unrealized. Proactive agents hold the promise to close this gap, but effective proactivity requires understanding users' real-time activities and personalizing intervention timing so that assistance is offered when it is likely to be helpful rather than disruptive. We introduce deep personalization, a closed-loop process that connects continuous context observation with continual agent adaptation: an agent observes user activity, selectively retains long-term context, and adapts intervention decisions from implicit feedback. We initiate deep personalization in Coco, a proactive desktop agent, by having a separate Observer Agent that monitors desktop activity, maintains compact long-term user modeling context, and learns when assistance is desirable from implicit signals such as engagement, dismissal, explicit requests, and repeated work patterns. We further develop efficient on-device strategies to enable the Observer Agent to run on local AI compute platforms without streaming raw activity data off-device. For evaluation at scale without collecting sensitive real-user activity, we introduce a data-synthesis pipeline that transforms existing computer-use trajectories into OSWorld-Persona, a benchmark with persona-conditioned intervention labels. On OSWorld-Persona, deep personalization improves macro F1 across three model backbones from 0.12-0.19 under direct prompting to 0.35-0.48 after adaptation, a 2.4-3.0x relative gain. In a week-long user study, we observe that Coco improves its macro-averaged F1 score from 0.199 to 0.515. These results suggest that on-device deep personalization can make proactive agents more timely and increasingly aligned with each user's everyday work.

</details>


### [148] [Leveraging Psychophysical Attentional Distribution for Gaze-Augmented Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13477)
*Chaeho Lee,Junyup Kim,Wansoo Kim,Young Min Jung,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在基于眼动数据训练RLHF奖励模型的基础上，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视点为中心的空间梯度分布，并用该表示训练奖励模型，实验表明其偏好预测准确率优于基线，且结果在定性上与人类阅读注意的认知心理学发现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类反馈（RLHF）用于对齐语言模型已受广泛关注，近期研究引入眼动（ET）数据作为奖励模型训练的额外监督信号，使奖励学习扎根于人类阅读行为。但已有方法以注视点为中心、较为简化，未充分利用认知心理学关于人类字母识别的成熟结论。作者希望更精细地刻画视觉注意的空间分布，从而提升奖励建模质量。

Method: 在先前以注视点为中心的模型基础上进行扩展：依据认知心理学中关于人类字母识别的既有发现，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视位置为中心、在空间上呈梯度衰减的分布（spatially graded distribution）；再把该注意分布表示引入奖励模型训练，作为偏好学习的监督信号。

Result: 采用所提出的注视分布训练的奖励模型，在偏好预测准确率（preference prediction accuracy）上高于基线模型；此外，实验中的部分结果在定性上与人类阅读过程中注意分配的既有发现相一致。

Conclusion: 将认知心理学启发的空间梯度注意分布纳入眼动监督的奖励模型训练是有效的，能够提升偏好预测表现，并可能捕捉到人类注意分配中具有认知意义的方面；但这主要体现为定性一致性，尚需更充分的验证。

Abstract: Integrating human feedback into language models has gained increasing attention as a means to align model outputs with human preferences, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) serving as a prominent framework for preference-based alignment. Recent studies have incorporated eye-tracking (ET) data as an additional supervisory signal for reward model training, grounding reward learning in human reading behavior. Building on this approach, we extend prior fixation-centric models by leveraging established findings from cognitive psychology on human letter recognition. Specifically, we model visual attention as a spatially graded distribution centered on the current gaze location and incorporate this representation into reward model training. Our results show that the reward model trained with the proposed gaze distribution achieves higher preference prediction accuracy than a baseline model. Moreover, some results from our experiments are qualitatively consistent with established findings on human attention during reading, suggesting that they potentially capture cognitively meaningful aspects of human attention allocation.

</details>


### [149] [Sharp Sample Complexity of KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13480)
*Andrew Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用"控制即推断"的图模型视角，证明KL正则化（最大熵/RLHF）强化学习中学习正则化最优策略（后验）的样本复杂度为 Õ(KH³/(ε·max{ε,β}) + KH)，并在 ε ≲ min{H, H²/β} 时给出匹配下界，同时给出plug-in估计量的精确渐近常数与最优采样分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化RL（最大熵RL、RLHF）本质是"控制即推断"图模型中的变分推断，其正则化最优策略是轨迹上的后验，策略次优度等于 β 倍路径测度间KL散度。然而即便有生成模型，学习该后验所需的样本数仍未知：已有 1/ε 保证在有效时域 H 上高达 H⁵ 的依赖，且MDP下界均不依赖温度 β。

Method: 针对有 K 个状态-动作对的折扣MDP：(1) 分析plug-in估计量（基于估计的soft Q函数构造策略）；(2) 推导样本复杂度上界 Õ(KH³/(ε·max{ε,β}) + KH)；(3) 构造下界，允许算法自适应选择采样位置；(4) 推导plug-in估计量的精确渐近常数——估计soft Q函数的Fisher加权方差，并证明其在等量采样方法中局部渐近极小极大；(5) 给出自适应设计下的最优样本分配；(6) 在小规模MDP上做仿真验证。

Result: Plug-in估计量可在 Õ(KH³/(ε·max{ε,β}) + KH) 样本下从每个初始状态得到 ε-最优策略；当 ε ≲ min{H, H²/β} 时，任何算法（含自适应采样）都无法用更少样本。当 ε < β 时达到 1/ε 速率，且时域依赖与无正则RL相同。精确渐近常数为Fisher加权方差；该估计量在等量采样方法中局部渐近极小极大，并给出了自适应设计下的最优分配。仿真结果与速率及常数吻合。

Conclusion: 本文建立了KL正则化RL后验学习的紧样本复杂度刻画：上界与下界在广泛 ε 区间内匹配，消除了此前 H⁵ 的多余时域依赖，首次给出依赖温度 β 的MDP下界，并给出渐近最优的常数与采样分配，说明正则化RL与无正则RL具有相同的时域复杂度阶。

Abstract: KL-regularized reinforcement learning, the objective behind maximum-entropy RL and RL from human feedback, is variational inference in the graphical model of "control as inference": the regularized optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories, and the suboptimality of a policy is $β$ times a KL divergence between path measures. However, it is not known how many samples it takes to learn this posterior, even with a generative model. Earlier $1/ε$ guarantees scale with $H^5$ in the effective horizon $H$, and no lower bound for MDPs depends on the temperature $β$. In this work, we show that for a discounted MDP with $K$ state-action pairs, the plug-in estimator finds a policy that is $ε$-optimal from every initial state with $\widetilde{O}(KH^3/(ε\max\{ε,β\}) + KH)$ samples, and that no algorithm, even one that chooses where to sample, can do with fewer when $ε\lesssim \min\{H, H^2/β\}$. For $ε< β$ this is a $1/ε$ rate with the same horizon dependence as unregularized RL. We also find the exact asymptotic constant of the plug-in estimator, a Fisher-weighted variance of the estimated soft Q-function. The estimator is locally asymptotically minimax among methods that sample every pair equally often, and we give the allocation of samples that is optimal among adaptive designs. Simulations on small MDPs match the rates and the constants.

</details>


### [150] [Evaluating Compositional Generalization in Transformers: The Role of Composition Equivalence and Module Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13498)
*Purva Pruthi,Andrew Yuan,Alexander D'Amour,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过系统评估揭示影响Transformer组合泛化的两个关键数据分布因素——组合等价性与模块覆盖，并说明基准设计中的相关缺陷会导致泛化能力被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于Transformer组合泛化的实证结论不一致，原因在于评估仅基于少量、临时设定的训练-测试划分，缺乏对分布因素的受控分析。

Method: 系统性地构造并评估多组训练-测试划分，重点控制两个分布因素："组合等价性"（不同模块序列归约为同一端到端函数）与"模块覆盖"（模块在训练与测试中出现在不同上下文）；对比仅学习最终输出的直接模型与学习中间及最终输出的逐步模型。

Result: 直接模型的高表现主要来自利用组合等价性（即模块序列可归约为相同端到端函数），消除等价性后性能显著下降，无法泛化到由已知模块构成的新端到端函数；模块覆盖偏移增大时性能单调下降，直接模型与逐步模型均受影响。在LiveCodeBench代码输出预测任务上，Llama3-8B的表现亦体现组合等价性的作用。

Conclusion: 含组合等价性的基准会显著高估直接模型的泛化能力；同时含模块覆盖违规与组合等价性的基准会加剧捷径学习。基准设计应显式控制这两类分布因素。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is an important form of out-of-distribution generalization, defined as the ability to train on some combinations of modules (e.g., functions such as sort, filter) and then generalize to unseen combinations. Despite substantial empirical work evaluating compositional generalization in transformers, findings have been inconsistent because evaluations were often conducted on a limited set of train-test splits, defined in an ad hoc manner. Through a systematic evaluation, we identify two key aspects of the train-test distribution that strongly affect performance: composition equivalence and module coverage. We show that the apparent performance of direct models (trained only on final outputs) results primarily from exploiting composition equivalences, meaning different sequences of modules that reduce to identical end-to-end functions. When benchmarks eliminate these equivalences, performance drops significantly, showing their inability to generalize to compositions of known modules that produce novel end-to-end functions. We further demonstrate that performance degrades monotonically as module coverage shifts increase (when modules appear in different contexts between train and test), affecting both direct and step-by-step models (trained on intermediate and final outputs). These findings have concrete implications for benchmark design: benchmarks that include compositional equivalences can lead to significant overestimation of the generalization abilities of direct models, and benchmarks that include both module coverage violations and compositional equivalences can exacerbate shortcut learning. As a case study, we demonstrate the role of compositional equivalence on the generalization performance of the pre-trained model Llama3-8B on the code-output prediction task in the coding benchmark LiveCodeBench.

</details>


### [151] [Parallel Broyden Methods for Efficiently Evaluating Nonlinear State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13523)
*Ian Christopher Tanoh,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于 Broyden 方法的拟牛顿求解器，用"对角+低秩"近似 Jacobian 块的耦合项，从轨迹割线构造近似，保持并行 scan 的可扩展性，在低秩 RNN、并行 MCMC 与扩散模型上迭代更少、在 Jacobian 昂贵或状态维度大时最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性状态空间模型通常需顺序求解，难以利用现代并行硬件。已有工作把求解重构为求根问题并用并行牛顿法求解，但精确牛顿法需乘稠密 Jacobian，代价高；基于对角近似的拟牛顿法虽可扩展，却因忽略状态维度间耦合而收敛缓慢。

Method: 提出基于 Broyden 方法的拟牛顿法：对 Jacobian 的各块采用"对角加低秩"近似以刻画耦合项；近似由轨迹割线构造，无需自动微分；通过秩压缩保持与并行 scan 的兼容性。

Result: 理论上证明在压缩 scan 下可有限步恢复精确轨迹；收敛性由压缩误差及沿当前轨迹误差方向的块近似误差刻画，而非谱范数。实验上，在低秩 RNN、并行 MCMC 算法与去噪扩散模型上，迭代次数少于其他拟牛顿法；当 Jacobian 求值昂贵或状态维度较大时，是最快的并行方法；在交互近似低秩时优势明显。

Conclusion: 对角加低秩的 Broyden 拟牛顿近似在保留对角方案扩展性的同时捕捉跨维度耦合，兼顾精度与速度，适用于 Jacobian 昂贵或状态维度大、交互近似低秩的场景。

Abstract: Nonlinear state space models are typically evaluated sequentially, limiting their ability to exploit modern parallel hardware. Recent work has shown that state space models can be evaluated in parallel by reformulating evaluation as a root-finding problem, which can be solved with a parallel form of Newton's method. However, exact Newton methods require expensive multiplication of dense Jacobian matrices, and quasi-Newton methods based on diagonal approximations, while more scalable, are often slow to converge because they neglect interactions across state dimensions. We introduce a novel quasi-Newton approach based on Broyden's method, which captures coupling terms with diagonal-plus-low-rank approximations to blocks of the Jacobian. We construct the approximations from trajectory secants, avoiding automatic differentiation while retaining the favorable scaling of diagonal approximations and remaining compatible with parallel scan through rank compression. We prove finite-step recovery of the exact trajectory under the compressed scan, and show that convergence is tracked by the compression error and by the block approximation error along the current trajectory error, rather than in spectral norm. Empirically, we show that our method is advantageous when interactions are approximately low rank or when Jacobian evaluation is expensive: on low-rank RNNs, parallel MCMC algorithms, and denoising diffusion models, it converges in fewer iterations than other quasi-Newton methods and is the fastest parallel method when Jacobian evaluation is expensive or state dimensions are large.

</details>


### [152] [The RSI Tax: When Recursive Self-Improvement Is Not Worth the Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13524)
*Yitong Zhang,Ang Li,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并量化"RSI Tax"（自改进过程的token成本），在连续任务流上对6种方法、2个语言模型进行端到端评估。结果表明，简单的有界笔记（bounded-note）基线在精度上匹敌或超过Meta-Harness、EvoSkill、RRSI，并节省65.6–71.0%的总token；且RSI Tax中74.8–93.6%来自验证开销，大量产物后续并未被使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自改进（RSI）用智能体自身任务经验提升性能，但其实际价值取决于性能增益能否覆盖"自改进+任务执行"的全部成本。现有工作多只报告精度提升，忽视了自改进过程本身的token开销，导致对RSI实用性的评估不完整。

Method: 在连续任务流上评估：每个任务在被其反馈用于后续更新之前先计分，保证评估无信息泄漏。定义RSI Tax为自改进过程的token成本，与任务执行成本一并计入端到端评估。对比6种方法与2个语言模型；将RSI Tax分解为验证（verification）、探索（exploration）、写作（writing）三类成本，并追踪相关状态/产物在后续任务中的实际使用情况。

Result: 有界笔记基线在观测精度上匹配或超过Meta-Harness、EvoSkill、RRSI，平均总token节省65.6–71.0%。对三种RSI方法，验证成本占RSI Tax的74.8–93.6%；相当比例的RSI Tax对应后续未被使用的状态或产物。

Conclusion: 昂贵的RSI流程存在实质性局限：部署智能体的性能提升未必能证明改进过程开销的合理性。应设计同时优化RSI Tax与期望任务执行成本、并保证智能体能力在任务流上持续增长的自改进目标。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) seeks to improve an agent using its own task experience. Its practical value depends on whether performance gains justify the full cost of self-improvement and task execution. We evaluate this trade-off on continual task streams, scoring each task before its feedback is used for subsequent updates. We define the \emph{RSI Tax} as the token cost of the self-improvement process and include it alongside task-execution costs in end-to-end evaluation. Across six methods and two language models, a bounded-note baseline matches or exceeds the observed accuracy of Meta-Harness, EvoSkill, and RRSI, with mean total-token savings of 65.6-71.0\%. We decompose the RSI Tax into verification, exploration, and writing costs, and track subsequent use of the associated state or artifact. For these three RSI methods, verification accounts for 74.8--93.6% of the aggregate RSI Tax. A substantial share of the RSI Tax is also associated with states or artifacts that are not used later in the evaluated streams. These results expose a practical limitation of costly RSI procedures: improvements in the deployed agent need not justify the expense of the improvement process. They motivate RSI objectives that jointly optimize the RSI Tax and expected task-execution cost while ensuring continued growth in agent capabilities across the task stream.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond the Log-Linear Frontier: Pre-Asymptotic Analysis of Finite-Budget Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13534)
*Tianyu Zhao,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究经典可分离幂律神经缩放律在有限预算下偏离log-线性前沿的现象，从谱异质性与域异质性两方面分析，并给出可计算的估计与分配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缩放律将超额风险建模为模型容量$N$与数据量$D$的可分离幂律，但在有限预算下，经验学习曲线与算力最优分配会偏离这种log-线性前沿，因此需要刻画并解释有限规模下的偏离机制。

Method: 容量轴：对协方差预解式做Mellin–Barnes分析，得到三项展开，竞争性谱模式与正则化模式产生有限尺度曲率与对数型过渡；因窄试点网格上拟合多幂律病态，用一阶Aitken差分消除损失下限，得到良态闭式初始化。数据轴：用KKT对偶将$K$域混合化为标量注水问题，给出有限预算瓶颈界、在线采样课程与用于跨域迁移的单纯形Hessian度量。等算力前沿$C=6ND$：证明最优分配指数漂移正比于容量与数据指数方差，并用Schur补约简分离内在漂移与冻结超参偏差。

Result: 在45个算子谱、Pythia（70M–12B）、Chinchilla（245次运行）与RegMix（17个域）上，估计器取得5.89%的留出集MAPE，将高算力分配遗憾最多降低76.4%，跨尺度混合RMSE降低59.4%，且选择遗憾为零。

Conclusion: 有限尺度偏离可归因于谱与域异质性：容量轴表现为多模式叠加的曲率与对数过渡，数据轴表现为注水型瓶颈约束，等算力前沿上的最优分配指数随指数方差漂移；所提估计与分配方法在多种模型与数据设定下显著优于基线且无选择遗憾。

Abstract: Classical neural scaling laws model excess risk as a separable power law in model capacity $N$ and dataset size $D$. At finite budgets, however, empirical learning curves and compute-optimal allocations depart from this log-linear frontier. This paper analyzes how spectral and domain heterogeneity govern these finite-scale departures. Along the capacity axis, Mellin--Barnes analysis of covariance resolvents yields a three-term expansion where competing spectral and regularization modes produce finite-scale curvature and logarithmic transitions. Because fitting multiple power laws on narrow pilot grids is ill-conditioned, we eliminate the loss floor via first-order Aitken differencing to obtain a well-conditioned closed-form initializer. Along the data axis, KKT duality reduces $K$-domain mixing to scalar water-filling, yielding finite-budget bottleneck bounds, an online sampling curriculum, and a simplex Hessian metric for cross-domain transfer. Along the iso-compute frontier $C=6ND$, we prove that the optimal allocation exponent drifts in proportion to capacity and data exponent variances, while Schur-complement reduction separates intrinsic drift from frozen-hyperparameter bias. Across 45 operator spectra, Pythia (70M--12B), Chinchilla (245 runs), and RegMix (17 domains), our estimators achieve $5.89\%$ holdout MAPE, cut high-compute allocation regret by up to $76.4\%$, and reduce cross-scale mixture RMSE by $59.4\%$ with zero selection regret.

</details>


### [154] [Adaptive catalyst discovery emerges from composable scientific intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13536)
*Wentao Li,Qilong Cai,Qi Lei,Jinxing Chen,Ruixuan Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Botian Wang,Kai Zhao,Honghao Chen,Leyi Zhao,Zemeng Wang,Yijun Li,Jun Yin,Fangfang Li,Hongxuan Liu,Yingdong Lu,Yawen Ouyang,Manu Suvarna,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Kostya S. Novoselov,Hao Zhou,Jiong Lu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可组合催化智能"（CatAI）——一个物理驱动的模块化AI框架，学习180万条结构-性质关系并组织为30个物理化学编码器，形成可复用的中间层，以7.8%的任务平均预测误差优势领先，并在工业均相聚合与Cu基双原子光催化C-O偶联两个体系中实现实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催化剂设计知识分散于实验观测、计算数据与各催化分支学科之间，缺乏统一、可复用的表示层，导致跨体系知识迁移困难。

Method: 构建物理驱动的模块化AI框架：从180万条结构-性质关系中学习，蒸馏为30个物理化学编码器（可复用中间层）；在此基础上构建任务特定模型用于定向反应与目标的催化剂筛选，并结合自主闭环优化与迭代式模型-实验反馈。

Result: 基准任务上任务平均预测误差比次优模型低7.8%；实验发现可穿越受限口袋的新催化剂，用于工业均相聚合，活性为商业基准的9倍；同时指导高活性Cu基双原子光催化剂的自主闭环优化，用于C-O偶联反应，性能超越传统Ni基体系。

Conclusion: 该方法将性质层面数据与催化目标相连接，通过计算筛选与迭代模型-实验反馈，实现物理化学知识在机理迥异体系间的复用，为模块化、可组合的催化剂发现提供范式。

Abstract: Catalysis underpins chemical manufacturing, yet knowledge for catalyst design remains dispersed across experimental observations, computational data and catalysis subdisciplines. To overcome this challenge, we present Composable Catalytic Intelligence (CatAI), a physics-grounded modular AI framework that learns 1.8 million structure-property relations and organizes them into 30 physicochemical encoders. This reusable intermediate layer enables task-specific models to guide catalyst discovery for targeted reactions and objectives. Across benchmark tasks, CatAI achieves a 7.8% lower task-averaged prediction error than the next-best models. In experimental validation, CatAI identified a new confined-passable pocket catalyst applicable in industrial homogeneous polymer synthesis with 9 times the activity of the commercial benchmark. In parallel, it guided autonomous closed-loop optimization of a highly active Cu-based geminal-atom photocatalyst for C-O coupling reactions surpassing the traditional Ni-based systems. This approach connects property-level data to catalytic objectives, supporting physicochemical knowledge reuse across mechanistically distinct systems through computational screening and iterative model-experiment feedback.

</details>


### [155] [Sanity Checks for Attention Surgery: Null Models and Matched Interventions for Representation-Motivated Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13543)
*Basavaraj R M,Aryan Bharat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出三项可复用检验（噪声地板、几何零假设、方向特异性），并以自注意力移除（self-attention removal）为对象，在9个预训练多头模型、5,408个头上验证其必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer结构改造常依据"某内部统计量大→从结构上移除它→报告性能提升"的推理链，但这一链条缺乏对统计量是否超出噪声地板、是否在几何零假设下仍成立、性能增益是否依赖被移除的特定方向的验证。

Method: 形式化三项检验并应用于self-attention removal（删除注意力头输出中与其自身value向量对齐的分量）；在5,408个头上引入早位因果零假设（earlier-position causal null）；在GQA下比较组内/组间对齐；用split-half可靠性评估单头干预效应的可分辨性；并开展预注册的3臂×8种子配对因子实验（每轮4.0×10^8 token，合计399,900,672 token）。

Result: 早位因果零假设可解释48%–63%的head-value余弦对齐，且无单调规模趋势；GQA下对齐具有组特异性（组内+0.24~+0.27，组间-0.11~-0.19）；干预效应可靠性在GPT-2为r_Δ=0.80，Pythia-160M/410M分别为0.42/0.53，而结构自余弦可靠性r_stat>0.99；训练中固定任意秩一方向与基线无差异（+0.0011 nats，p=0.197），自方向移除降低验证损失（-0.0029 nats，Holm校正p=0.0023）并优于固定任意方向（-0.0040 nats，p=0.0018）。

Conclusion: 在50.9M规模下训练收益确实依赖自身value方向而非固定任意方向，但仅凭大的余弦对齐不足以在冻结模型中确立冗余性；哪一统计量能预测其自身干预效果具有模型依赖性。

Abstract: Transformer architectural modifications frequently measure a statistic on internal representations, observe that it is large, remove it architecturally, and report an improvement. Interpreting that sequence requires evaluating whether the statistic exceeds its noise floor, whether it survives a geometric null, and whether the training gain requires the specific direction removed. We formulate these requirements as three reusable checks and apply them to self-attention removal, which deletes the component of an attention head's output aligned with its own value vector. Across nine multi-head pretrained models (5,408 heads, 124M to 6.9B parameters), an earlier-position causal null accounts for 48% to 63% of the head-value cosine alignment, with no monotone scale trend. Under grouped-query attention, this alignment is specific to each head's own key-value group and reverses across groups (+0.24 to +0.27 within-group, -0.11 to -0.19 across-group). Split-half reliability demonstrates that the per-head intervention effect is resolvable in GPT-2 ($r_Δ= 0.80$) but attenuated in Pythia-160M (0.42) and Pythia-410M (0.53), whereas structural self-cosine reliability is near-perfect in all three ($r_{\text{stat}} > 0.99$); which statistic predicts its own intervention is model-dependent. In a pre-registered 3-arm by 8-seed paired factorial at $4.0 \times 10^8$ tokens per run (399,900,672 tokens), a fixed arbitrary rank-one direction does not differ from baseline (+0.0011 nats, $p = 0.197$), while self-directed removal lowers validation loss (-0.0029 nats, Holm-corrected $p = 0.0023$) and outperforms the fixed arbitrary control directly (-0.0040 nats, $p = 0.0018$). At 50.9M scale the training benefit requires the own-value direction rather than a fixed arbitrary one, but a large cosine alignment alone does not establish redundancy in frozen models.

</details>


### [156] [Closing the BF16-FP16 Convergence Speed Gap in RL Post-Training with Stochastic Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13545)
*Mahdi Nazemi,Oleg Rybakov,Tijmen Blankevoort,Asit Mishra,Carlo del Mundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出在带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，BF16混合精度训练因"权重粘滞"（weight sticking）而收敛慢于FP16，并提出在FP32影子权重量化到BF16时采用随机舍入（stochastic rounding）来消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BF16因动态范围等同FP32、收敛性接近FP32而被广泛用于LLM预训练。但在RLVR场景下学习率通常较低，作者发现标准BF16混合精度训练在相同学习率下收敛速度慢于FP16，需要找出原因并修复，同时保留BF16相对FP16的固有优势（更宽动态范围、无需loss scaling）。

Method: 分析原因：将差距归因于"权重粘滞"——FP32影子权重用round-to-nearest量化为BF16时，小幅更新常无法改变用于rollout生成与训练的BF16权重，导致需多个优化器步才能体现更新。对比方案一：提高BF16学习率可缓解粘滞并追平FP16收敛速度，但会增大相对参考模型的KL散度。方案二（本文采用）：在FP32影子权重→BF16量化时使用随机舍入。

Result: 随机舍入在多个模型和数据集上达到与FP16相当的收敛速度，且相比"提高学习率的BF16训练"具有更低的KL散度。同时保持BF16更宽的动态范围，无需loss scaling。

Conclusion: 随机舍入是一种简单有效的修正，可解决低学习率下BF16混合精度在RLVR中的权重粘滞问题，兼顾FP16级的收敛速度与更低的KL偏移，并保留BF16的工程优势。

Abstract: BF16 is widely used to pretrain large language models because it has the same dynamic range as FP32 and converges similarly to FP32 training. However, in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where learning rates are typically low, we find that standard mixed-precision BF16 training converges more slowly than FP16 training at the same learning rate. We attribute this gap to weight sticking: when FP32 shadow weights are quantized to BF16 using round-to-nearest, small updates often leave the BF16 weights used for rollout generation and training unchanged. Consequently, several optimizer steps may be needed before updates to the shadow weights are reflected in the BF16 weights. Raising the BF16 learning rate reduces weight sticking and matches FP16's convergence speed, but it also increases the KL divergence from the reference model. We instead apply stochastic rounding when quantizing the FP32 shadow weights to BF16. This approach matches FP16's convergence speed across different models and datasets while reducing the KL divergence relative to BF16 training with a larger learning rate. Unlike FP16, it keeps BF16's wider dynamic range and needs no loss scaling.

</details>


### [157] [Massive Activations Confound Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13548)
*Nathan Delisle,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）的常用评测指标（解释方差、消融损伤）会被"巨型激活"严重污染；本文指出问题机理并提出"去除PC1训练再加回"的修正方法，显著提升特征稳定性和评测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE评测依赖解释方差与消融损伤，但作者发现巨型激活（massive activations）会同时破坏这两项指标：高达99.9%的激活方差集中于第一主成分（PC1），而PC1缺乏唯一的稀疏分解，导致不同训练运行用不同特征集去重建它，特征重要性排名极不稳定，评测结果失真。

Method: （1）诊断：量化PC1方差占比、跨运行特征匹配率、PC1外方差的解释缺口；（2）修正：在去除PC1的激活上训练SAE，再在重建时把PC1加回，使字典不必承担不可唯一分解的PC1成分。

Result: 原始设置下，top-50重要特征中平均47个在另一次运行中无匹配（随机50个约27个）；词典可报告99.96%解释方差却有33%的PC1外方差未被解释。采用修正后，受影响模型的top-50特征共享率由4–39.5%升至73–99.5%；相同TopK稀疏度下，模型损伤最多降低44%，内容重建误差最多降低33.9%。

Conclusion: 巨型激活是SAE评测与可解释性分析的系统性混杂因素，PC1不可唯一稀疏分解是特征不稳定的根源；移除PC1训练、重建时加回是简单有效的修正，可同时改善特征稳定性、解释方差真实性与模型保真度。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are commonly evaluated by variance explained and ablation damage. We show how massive activations undermine both evaluations, and propose a correction. Massive activations cause up to 99.9% of activation variance to lie along the first principal component (PC1). Because the PC1 component has no unique sparse decomposition, each SAE run rebuilds it from a different set of features. These features dominate importance rankings: an average of 47 of the 50 most important features are unmatched in a separate run, compared with about 27 of a random 50. A dictionary can also report 99.96% of variance explained while leaving 33% of the variance outside PC1 unexplained. We bypass PC1 by training the SAE on activations with PC1 removed, then adding PC1 to the reconstruction. In affected models, the top-50 feature sharing rate rises from 4-39.5% to 73-99.5%. At the same TopK sparsity, the resulting SAE degrades the model up to 44% less and reduces content reconstruction error by up to 33.9%.

</details>


### [158] [Tabular Foundation Models in Financial Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13550)
*Ed deHaan,Allen H. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍表格基础模型（TFMs）并用于会计研究中的企业次年盈余变动预测；TabFM在精度上优于微调开源大语言模型及若干树模型，且比LLM更快、算力更省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会计与金融预测领域需要易用、无需训练调优的预测工具；同时需避免预训练模型记忆企业结果导致的look-ahead bias（前视偏差）。

Method: 将TFM引入会计研究；TFM在合成表格数据上预训练，通过上下文学习直接输出数值预测，无需发布后训练或调参；以预测企业次年盈余变动为基准任务，将Google Research的TabFM与近期会计研究中微调的开源权重大语言模型及多种树模型进行比较。

Result: TabFM精度高于所有对比模型；相比大语言模型速度更快、计算开销更低；部分树模型设定预测速度快于TabFM，故存在精度与速度的权衡。

Conclusion: TFM有望成为会计与金融预测的新工具；但评估仅基于单一基准任务，且不太可能有某一类模型在所有情境下占优，模型选择需结合实际场景权衡。

Abstract: This technical note introduces tabular foundation models (TFMs) to accounting research and evaluates their performance in one common benchmark task: predicting firms' next-year earnings changes. TFMs produce numeric predictions and learn in context, without post-release training or tuning, making them natural and easy to use for forecasting. The TFMs we study are pretrained on synthetic tabular data, so they avoid look-ahead bias from memorizing firms' outcomes. We compare Google Research's TabFM with a fine-tuned open-weight large language model developed in recent accounting research and with several tree-based models. TabFM is more accurate than all comparison models. It is also faster and less computationally intensive than the large language model. Several tree-based specifications produce predictions more quickly than TabFM, so choosing between them involves a trade-off between accuracy and speed. We conclude that TFMs have promise as a new tool for forecasting in accounting and finance. However, our evaluation is based on a single benchmark task, and no class of models is likely to dominate in all settings.

</details>


### [159] [Discovering Structurally Plausible and Interpretable Cognitive Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13561)
*Boyoung Kwon,Jiyoon Seo,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DisCo：LLM 引导、以进化搜索发现可解释符号认知模型的框架，含 Validator（过滤结构无效模型）与 Cleaner（删除冗余参数以提升参数可辨识性）两个结构组件；在三个行为任务上性能与任务专用基线相当，消融表明 Validator 对恢复具心理学意义的结构至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算认知建模依赖人工构建，耗时且需大量领域专业知识；现有基于 LLM 的自动化方法偏重预测精度，牺牲了结构合理性与参数可辨识性。

Method: LLM 引导的进化搜索生成符号化认知模型；Validator 过滤结构上无效（违反行为约束）的模型；Cleaner 通过去除冗余参数提升参数可辨识性。

Result: 在三个行为任务上，DisCo 发现的模型性能与任务专用基线相比具有竞争力；消融分析显示移除 Validator 后模型倾向违反行为约束，且与外部认知测量的关联更不一致，说明该组件对恢复心理学有意义结构很重要。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的模型发现若结合恰当的结构约束，可得到超越预测拟合、具认知合理性的模型。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling has traditionally relied on manual model construction, which is a time-intensive process that requires substantial domain expertise. Although recent LLM-based approaches have begun to automate model generation, they often prioritize predictive accuracy at the expense of structural plausibility and parameter identifiability. We introduce DisCo, an LLM-guided framework that discovers interpretable symbolic cognitive models through evolutionary search with two structural components: a Validator that filters structurally invalid models, and a Cleaner that promotes parameter identifiability by removing redundant parameters. Across three behavioral tasks, DisCo consistently discovers models that achieve performance competitive with task-specific baselines. Ablation analyses show that the Validator is important for recovering psychologically meaningful structure. Without this component, models tend to violate behavioral constraints and show less consistent associations with external cognitive measures. These results suggest that LLM-driven model discovery, when coupled with appropriate structural constraints, can yield cognitively plausible models that go beyond predictive fit.

</details>


### [160] [Investigating Composite Modeling in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13565)
*Ricky Ye,Sahas Goli,Muhammad Ali,Poompol Buathong,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究复合建模（学习中 间响应）在多目标贝叶斯优化中是否带来增益，并结合理论条件与数值实验给出结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标贝叶斯优化中目标评估昂贵；已有单目标工作表明利用中间响应的复合建模能显著提升采样效率，但其在 MOBO 中的收益尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 1) 在单目标设定下，推导中间观测相对目标值不提供额外预测信息（在相同 warped GP 先验下复合与黑箱等价）的条件；2) 在 8 个合成与应用启发的科学设计基准上，对 4 种 MOBO 方法的复合版本与黑箱版本（标准多输出 GP）进行受控数值实验，考察上述条件与多目标增益的关系。

Result: 两类研究共同表明：当中间响应到最终目标的映射为非双射且非仿射时，复合建模的收益更大。

Conclusion: 复合建模在 MOBO 中的增益取决于中间响应到目标的映射性质，非双射、非仿射映射是获得显著提升的关键情形。

Abstract: Scientific design often requires balancing competing objectives that are expensive to evaluate through experiments or simulations. Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) addresses this challenge by using probabilistic models to guide the selection of promising designs. In many applications, tunable inputs map to observable intermediate responses, which are then combined through known functions to compute the final objectives. Prior work on Bayesian optimization of composite functions shows that learning from these intermediate responses, which we call Composite modeling, can substantially improve sampling efficiency in single-objective optimization, compared to methods that condition only on black-box observations of the objective function. The benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO, however, remain understudied. In this work, we study the benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO and investigate the factors driving variation in its gains. We first establish single-objective conditions under which intermediate observations provide no additional predictive information beyond objective values, where composite and black box methods use the same warped Gaussian process prior. Controlled numerical experiments with versions of four MOBO methods across eight synthetic and application-inspired scientific-design benchmarks examine how these conditions relate to optimization gains in the multi-objective setting, where the black box approach models the objective functions with a standard multi-output Gaussian process. Both investigations support the claim that composite modeling tends to help more when the function mapping intermediate responses to final objectives is non-bijective and non-affine.

</details>


### [161] [Metamorphic testing of learned AC optimal power flow surrogates reveals representation dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13566)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用13条变形关系审计17种AC-OPF代理模型/流水线配置，发现每种配置至少违反6条关系，即便把容差放宽或收紧10倍亦然；定位到GridSFM-Open v1.1的索引错误与三项非规范/数值不稳定的预处理，并通过case500实验显示数据增强的收益依赖训练协议、且预测仍全部AC不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅在单一电网序列化编码上评估学习型AC-OPF代理，无法判断其精度是否依赖于该编码，因此需要检验模型对表示变换的依赖性。

Method: 设计13条变形关系，每条明确规定变换的适用条件与输出比较方式；审计17种模型与流水线配置（2个已发布训练检查点与15个随机权重架构/机制对照）；对GridSFM-Open v1.1进行源码检查与中间张量干预；另开展case500训练实验考察数据增强效应。

Result: 所有配置在报告容差的10倍收紧与10倍放宽下均至少违反6条关系。GridSFM-Open v1.1的问题源于一处索引错误及三项非规范或数值不稳定的预处理选择（修复版检查点尚未测试）。case500增强实验中，4个模型里3个降低了变换视图误差，4个模型全部降低中位差异与约束违反P95，但部分关系变差，且所有预测仍AC不可行；原始拉普拉斯基线的大幅提升主要来自GPU/CPU特征求解器不匹配。

Conclusion: 建议在报告精度、可行性与成本之外，同时报告表示一致性；这些结果既不证明可部署性，也不构成普遍不变性。

Abstract: Evaluating a learned AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) surrogate on one grid serialization cannot show whether its accuracy depends on that encoding. We test this dependence with thirteen metamorphic relations, each specifying when a transformation is valid and how to compare its outputs. Our audit covers 17 model and pipeline configurations: two released trained checkpoints and 15 random-weight architecture or mechanism controls. Every configuration violates at least six relations, even at reporting tolerances ten times tighter or looser. For GridSFM-Open v1.1, source inspection and intermediate-tensor interventions point to an indexing bug and three noncanonical or numerically unstable preprocessing choices; a repaired checkpoint remains untested. In a separate case500 training experiment, augmentation lowers transformed-view error in three of four models and median discrepancy and constraint-violation P95 in all four. These benefits depend on the training protocol: some relations worsen, and every evaluated prediction remains AC infeasible. A GPU/CPU eigensolver mismatch accounts for most of the large raw-Laplacian-baseline gain. We recommend reporting representation consistency alongside accuracy, feasibility, and cost. These results establish neither deployment readiness nor universal invariance.

</details>


### [162] [Market-Conditioned JEPA: Learning from Unpredictable Market Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13572)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Lei Li,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Market-Conditioned JEPA（MC-JEPA），在潜在因子模型下将市场因子作为条件引入联合嵌入预测架构，用其他资产未来嵌入构造留一法市场条件、并通过资产自身表征导出的因子载荷注入，在中国 A 股 11 年分钟级数据上全面优于逐资产 JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列的很大一部分变异来自无法由资产自身历史预测的市场级冲击，而逐资产自监督学习把这种变异当作不可约噪声处理，导致市场暴露、风险与流动性类目标学习效率低。作者希望显式利用可实现的市场因子来分离市场共同成分与资产特有成分。

Method: 基于潜在因子模型的理论分析表明：以可实现市场因子为条件时，资产的因子载荷（市场暴露）成为最优预测的必要量，同时降低资产特有成分学习所面对的噪声。据此构建 MC-JEPA：训练时用其他资产未来目标嵌入构造每资产的 leave-one-out 市场条件，并经由资产自身表征得到的因子载荷注入条件；预测器恢复归一化去除的波动尺度后可直接用于 beta 排序。

Result: 在中国 A 股 11 年分钟级数据上，MC-JEPA 在收益、市场暴露、风险、流动性四类共九个目标上均优于逐资产 JEPA，其中暴露、风险、流动性的提升最强且统计显著；在全部流动性目标和 2 小时至 5 天收益周期上取得最佳 RankIC，超过时间序列基础模型与自监督基线，历史回测组合表现最佳。消融显示收益与暴露增益要求条件与可实现市场走势对齐；恢复波动尺度后，预测器因子载荷在无标签情况下对市场 beta 的排序能力接近监督探针；已知参数的合成因子模型实验表明条件化同时改善市场暴露与资产特有成分的恢复。

Conclusion: 显式条件化于可实现市场因子是提升金融时间序列自监督表征的关键，可同时提升预测精度与因子结构可解释性，并在实盘回测中转化为组合收益。

Abstract: Much of the variation in financial time series is driven by market-wide shocks that cannot be predicted from an asset's own history. Per-asset self-supervised learning treats this variation as irreducible noise. Under a latent factor model, we show that conditioning prediction on the realized market factor makes the asset's market exposure necessary for optimal prediction, while reducing the noise against which the asset-specific component is learned. Building on this insight, we introduce Market-Conditioned JEPA (MC-JEPA), a joint-embedding predictive architecture for financial time series. During training, we construct a leave-one-out market condition for each asset from the future target embeddings of other assets and incorporate it through factor loadings derived from the asset's own representation. On 11 years of minute-level Chinese A-share data, MC-JEPA improves over per-asset JEPA across nine targets spanning returns, market exposure, risk, and liquidity, with the strongest and most statistically significant gains in exposure, risk, and liquidity. It achieves the best RankIC on all liquidity targets and on return horizons from two hours to five days, outperforming time-series foundation models and self-supervised baselines, and delivers the best portfolio performance in a historical backtest. Ablations show that the return and exposure gains require the condition to be aligned with the realized market movement. After restoring the volatility scale removed by normalization, the predictor's factor loadings rank stocks by market beta nearly as well as a supervised probe, without labels. A synthetic factor-model study with known parameters shows that conditioning improves the recovery of both market exposure and the asset-specific component.

</details>


### [163] [Unified Optimization of Traffic Signal and Lane Channelization for an Isolated Intersection Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13599)
*Jiwon Kang,Jiyoung Kim,Hyunseok Jun,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于深度Q网络（DQN）的强化学习方法，实时联合优化信号配时与车道渠化（可变导向车道）设计，并用人类反馈规则过滤危险的动作切换，实验证明可提升交叉口通行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号控制是实时动态问题，而车道渠化是静态设计问题，二者难以联合求解；已有MILP联合模型在流量模式变化时需反复求解，且规模增大时计算时间不可接受，故需一种实时可扩展的联合优化方法。

Method: 将联合优化建模为强化学习问题；设计可容纳"信号相位+车道标线方案"联合动作的深度Q网络（DQN）；考虑车道标线与信号相位不兼容会带来安全风险，采用基于人类反馈训练的模型，以规则方式过滤不可行动作转移（infeasible action transitions）；实施上需在每个停止线设置可变车道标志。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法通过提高通行量（throughput）改善了交叉口运行性能，并能在实时条件下给出联合优化方案。

Conclusion: 基于RL的联合优化可替代需反复求解的MILP，实现信号与车道渠化的实时协同决策；引入人类反馈的动作过滤机制保障了动作切换的安全性；该方案依赖可变车道标志的硬件部署。

Abstract: Optimizing traffic signal control has been a long-lasting critical issue in traffic engineering, and a number of robust methodologies have emerged to solve the problem so far. On the other hand, the road design for lane channelization is a static problem that is difficult to solve jointly with the real-time traffic signal control. If traffic volume does not vary, it is possible that both problems can be solved in an integrated manner as a single static optimization problem. Recently, researchers have devised a mixed integer linear problem (MILP) to jointly determine the traffic signal and road width designs. However, this joint optimization problem must be solved repeatedly whenever the traffic flow pattern varies. Solving the MILP may also take impractical computing time as the problem size gets larger. We present a robust methodology for solving the joint optimization problem using reinforcement learning (RL) on a real-time basis. Abrupt changes in lane markings with an incompatible signal phase can jeopardize drivers. To sidestep such a dangerous action change, we train a model with human feedback to filter infeasible action transitions in a rule-based manner. We devised a deep Q-network that can accommodate joint actions for traffic signal and lane marking plans. To ensure the implementation of the proposed operation, a variable sign for lane markings is necessary at each stop line of an intersection. The experimental results confirm that the proposed methodology enhances the intersection performance by increasing throughput.

</details>


### [164] [Multifidelity Operator Inference for Non-intrusive Model Reduction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13624)
*Vivian Zhang,Tomoki Koike,Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真算子推断(MF-OpInf)，用同问题粗网格的低保真数据补足稀缺的高保真快照，通过多保真线性回归框架估计降维算子，从而降低方差，在相同计算预算下比仅用高保真数据的OpInf更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多查询分析（不确定性量化、优化设计）中高保真PDE模型计算代价过高；代理模型可替代昂贵仿真，而ML代理（如OpInf）无需接触仿真代码，属非侵入式。但受计算预算限制，高保真训练数据稀缺，导致OpInf模型方差大，这在高风险工程设计中是致命缺陷。

Method: Multifidelity Operator Inference：将稀缺高保真快照与同一问题更粗离散化产生的大量低保真数据结合；利用多保真线性回归框架处理不同保真度数据，以估计降维算子。

Result: 在模型PDE问题上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，MF-OpInf对PDE解场的预测精度高于仅使用高保真数据的OpInf。

Conclusion: 多保真数据融合能有效降低OpInf代理模型的方差并提升精度，为预算受限下的工程不确定性量化与设计提供可行路径。

Abstract: Many-query analyses, in which a computational model is repeatedly evaluated, play an important role in uncertainty quantification and optimization for engineering design. However, many-query analyses can be prohibitively expensive when considering high-fidelity numerical models, especially those governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). Surrogate modeling replaces costly simulations with cheap approximations, enabling practical uncertainty quantification and design workflows. Machine learning (ML) approaches to surrogate modeling have gained significant traction in the development of surrogate models due to their non-intrusive nature; they do not require access to the underlying simulation code. The Operator Inference (OpInf) approach, in particular, fits reduced operators to state and corresponding time derivative data.
